Yin Qing and his three classmates walked a little closer to the inside of the room, and followed the merchants’ example. They moved two tables to the corners, laying them down, and placed their book packs to one side.

The remaining tables and benches could be tilted on their sides to block the wind, or used as frames to raise them off the ground if the weather was cold and damp. If they were in a spot of difficulty, they could be chopped up and used as firewood.

“Mo Xiu, let’s go collect some firewood, it’s very cold in the mountains at night.”

Yin Qing suggested to his companions, and then turned to Lei Yusheng and Lin Xinjie, who seemed to want to speak.

“You guys can tidy up around here, clear up a small area or move a few more tables over so we can sleep well at night.”

Hearing this, Lin Xinjie frowned.

“Why go to all this trouble? Didn’t we ask our carriage driver to buy us a hatchet? Couldn’t we just chop up the table sand chairs here and use them as firewood?”

“Yeah, we’re exhausted after walking for most of the day, and it’s going to rain soon, it’s so troublesome to go back out!”

Yin Qing squatted down, took out the hatchet from his pack, smiled, and shook his head.

“Haven’t you wondered why there are still so many tables and chairs in this deserted outpost? If everyone chopped the items here and used them for firewood, it would be quite inconvenient for people to rest here in the future! Unless it’s absolutely necessary, it’s best not to destroy anything. It’s not raining or dark yet, so let’s go out and get some dry firewood…”

When he finished speaking, Yin Qing approached Lei Yusheng and Lin Xinjie and spoke in a low voice.

“Look after our packs.”

“Well, let’s go!”

“Yeah, the sooner you go, the sooner you’ll come back!”

Yin Qing picked up the hatchet, called out for Mo Xiu to follow, and walked out. For one thing, he wanted the other two men to look after their packs. Furthermore, those two were true young masters who had never done any menial labor since childhood; they had walked for a long time today and were exhausted.

Mo Xiu at least still had some physical strength. Although Yin Qing himself was also tired, he could be considered the most physically fit individual of their group.

When the traveling merchants over to the side overheard Yin Qing’s words, a few of the older ones turned their heads and looked at him intently. Although they didn’t say anything, their impression of this scholar had obviously greatly improved.

As Yin Qing and Mo Xiu left the deserted waystation, they could feel the wind starting to blow harder outside. They looked up at the dark clouds, which seemed to be even closer to some of the surrounding tall peaks. They could hear the rumbling sound of thunder from time to time.

“Let’s go up the flatter slope nearby.”

“Mhm!”

The two rolled up their sleeves, tied their cuffs with straps and rope they had taken out from their packs, and went to the nearby hillside.

There was no shortage of firewood in the mountains. Yin Qing and Mo Xiu picked up a lot of branches of different sizes, and also cut some from the trees. They used the hatchet to chop some of the branches, and did their best to tie them into bundles with their ropes. It only took them a quarter of an hour to collect a small bundle.

“Boom…”

The thunder in the sky became louder and the wind picked up. Mo Xiu was still collecting more firewood, but Yin Qing had stopped.

“Mo Xiu, stop gathering firewood. It’s going to rain soon, we need to go back quickly!”

“Huh? How can just this little bit be enough?”

“Hey, if we don’t go back soon, we’ll be soaked to the bone. Getting soaked in the mountains is no joke!”

The two men grabbed their haul of firewood and walked along the mountainside. If they found any dead branches or logs along their path, Mo Xiu would pick them up and put them on top of their bundle.

When they arrived back at the deserted outpost, the rotting wooden door had been half-closed, leaving only a small gap between it and the frame.

“Squeak…”

The two pushed it open together, and the old wooden door squeaked as it ground against the floor. Lei Yusheng and Lin Xinjie on the other side hurriedly stood up and went over to help bring the firewood in, before closing the door.

Only then did Yin Qing and Mo Xiu finally hear the loud whooshing noises from outside the door. It seemed that the wind had already grown quite strong.

“Young man, if you don’t have enough firewood, we have some extra here. It’s all dry firewood. If you need kindling, come over and take some charcoal.”

Seeing the four scholars hurrying about, an old man surnamed Lu of the traveling merchants spoke to them.

The traveling merchants were no large corporate businessman; their money was earned through hard labor. They would prepare everything on their own whenever they traveled. If there was firewood around, they would chop that and start a fire; if there wasn’t, they would always carry dry firewood in their packs.

The four scholars were immediately surprised and thanked him repeatedly.

About half an hour later, the rain finally started to come down. Fortunately, although the waystation had long been abandoned, there weren’t too many leaks, and most of the ones that did exist were in the corners, near the outside of the walls. Yin Qing and the traveling merchants were well-situated.

Two piles of firewood were burning in the central room of the waystation. Yin Qing used a few earthen bricks that he found in the outpost as a makeshift stove, and copied some of the traveling merchants, who used pots to collect rainwater and brought it back to boil.

When it rained in the mountains, it grew darker much faster. The traveling merchants were obviously well prepared. The ones sitting on the inside were warming themselves by the fire, while those sitting on the outside wore straw raincoats and hats. They also set up their surrounding tables and benches.

There were fewer people on Yin Qing’s side of the room, so it wasn’t as cold when they wanted to warm themselves by the fire. They followed the merchants’ example and prepared some dry food, sticking it on wooden sticks to make roasted buns.

“This night won’t be that difficult to endure, but I might not be able to sleep.”

Lei Yusheng spoke. He took a book from his book pack, and began to read it by the firelight.

About two hours later, Yin Qing, Mo Xiu, and Lin Xinjie still sat by the fire, wide-eyed and staring at each other, and Lei Yusheng had fallen asleep, curled up on a low table with his book as a pillow.

“I really admire Yusheng a little…”

Lin Xinjie said with a twitch of his lips. Beside him, Mo Xiu was also dumbfounded.

“Didn’t he just say that he won’t be able to sleep tonight?”

Many of the traveling merchants on the other side were already lying down as well. Some were even snoring, obviously sleeping soundly. However, there were essentially always a few people awake, and the ones keeping watch chatted in low voices.

“Uncle Lu, where do you think those scholars are from? Their accents don’t sound like people from Wan Province.”

Someone glanced over to the other side of the room and asked curiously.

“It’s not too clear. Anyway, it’s none of our business.”

“A scholar can become an official if he passes the imperial examination. Hey, Uncle Lu, when do you think I’ll get rich? When can I go to school, pass the imperial examination, become a top scholar, and live a life of wealth and honor for the rest of my life?”

The older man surnamed Lu looked at him, and then back at the scholars to the side.

“Probably soon. Someone will come over and take your watch in a little while.”1

The man scratched his head. He didn’t quite understand Lu Bo’s words, and felt that he was just talking nonsense. 2 However, Yin Qing, who heard it clearly, burst out laughing.

The older man surnamed Lu then sighed at his companion.

“Ah, that’s the difference between your brains!”

At this very moment, there was a sudden knock at the door of the deserted outpost.

“Knock knock knock… knock knock knock…”

“Is there anyone there? We just spotted a fire!”

The high-pitched voice and knocking on the door woke all the sleeping merchants.

“The door isn’t blocked, just push it open!”

As soon as one of the merchants finished speaking, someone pushed the door open from outside. The wooden door grated against the ground as it was shoved open.

“Whooo… whooooo…”

“Shhhhh…plik plik plik…”3

It was stormy outside.

“Boom…”

“Aaaah!”

As lighting illuminated the door, a woman screamed, and several figures at the door hurried into the waystation, pushing the door closed again.

By the light of the fire inside, the traveling merchants and the scholars could see that it was a group of three women. Although they had umbrellas, they were obviously soaked through. They were rubbing their arms and looked very cold. Some parts of their clothes clung to their bodies, revealing graceful figures.

“Shall we warm ourselves by the fire?”

The leading woman glanced at both sides of the room and asked.

As for the traveling merchants, although most of them were staring at the women’s bodies and features, almost all of them had their axes in hand, and didn’t say anything unnecessary.

The scholars were also looking at the women, Yin Qing and Lei Yusheng included, despite the latter having just woken up. However, Yin Qing, the youngest, had received strict education from his teacher, and did not look at them too directly.

The three women naturally walked over to the scholars.

Lei Yusheng hurriedly got off the table and sat upright beside the fire, while Lin Xinjie brought over a stool and dusted it down with a towel.

“Young man, you are all scholars, men and women should not mingle!”

The elder surnamed Lu suddenly spoke these words: age bred wisdom, after all.4 No matter how one looked at it, it was quite strange for three women to suddenly appear in the wilderness in the middle of the night.

Yin Qing immediately followed suit.

“The Old Man just reminded me, these girls were soaked by the rain and need a separate place to warm themselves by the fire. By the way, Old Man, how about we give our campfire to the girls? As scholars, we should not look upon them without their permission. How about we squeeze in with you? Brother Mo, you agree with me, right?”

The other three looked at Yin Qing, but hesitated and didn’t answer.

“That’s right, that’s right. Then, come on over here, youngsters!”

Yin Qing bowed to the old man, glanced sternly at his friends, and forcibly dragged them over to the other side.

“Oh… we don’t care…”

One woman said in a daze.

“But we care! A girl’s reputation is no trivial matter!”

Yin Qing dragged his friends to the merchants’ side without saying anything more, or even taking his book pack along. At this moment, his strength was so powerful that the three scholars who wanted to dawdle were dragged over by him alone.

The three women were obviously a little stunned. After a long moment, the leader laughed, and jokingly said, “what a pedant!”5 and led the other two women to sit by the campfire that the scholars had previously been at. They subconsciously looked at the four book packs.

On the other side, the elder surnamed Lu nodded to Yin Qing and others. He gestured them over to sit at the empty seats near the campfire, and spoke in a very low voice.

“When you go out, it’s better to be safe than sorry!”6

Yin Qing just bowed with cupped hands and didn’t speak. Unlike the others, he seemed to be able to smell a pungent odor, which made him extremely uneasy, especially since these women all appeared to be human.

Over the past couple months, in addition to studying by the riverbank, he had occasionally chatted with the Old Turtle in the river when no one else was around. The Old Turtle of the Chunmu River said that although Dazhen was peaceful, there were actually countless tales of mountain spirits and monsters in this world. However, regardless of their relative strength, as long as they had successfully cultivated into a true human body, their cultivation would definitely not be weak.

Of course, when considering the human transformation, the “cultivation” element was essential; some monsters’ human bodies were created by spiritual powers and techniques, and they remained essentially unchanged at their core, even if mortals could not see through their disguise.

Then, there were other various tricks to lure mortals, such as “deceptive sacrifice,” in which they would steal the essence or vitality of human beings, which could shorten some people’s lifespans and even kill others.

As the saying went, “those who handle cinnabar are stained red; those who work with ink are stained black.”7 Yin Qing had been exposed to similar things before, and instinctively felt that there was something wrong with these three women.

With a “bang!” one of the book packs fell over, and the inkstone, wolf hair brush, and several other items inside fell out.

“Aiya… Whose things are these? I8 accidentally knocked it over!”

One of the women looked anxious.

“It’s mine, it’s mine! Hey, don’t step on the rice paper!”

“Ah… don’t step on it!”

Mo Xiu was in a hurry and ran over quickly. Yin Qing shouted, “Mo Xiu!” but it was too late to hold him back.

However, nothing happened when Mo Xiu arrived. The woman apologized repeatedly and helped him pack the book pack back up. When she saw Mo Xiu’s clothes in the pack, the woman became timid.

“Sir, my clothes are wet, and I feel cold and uncomfortable. Can I borrow your clothes to wear?”

“Huh? Uh… oh…”

Mo Xiu glanced at the woman’s clothes, which clung to her body. His face grew a little red, and he opened his book pack to get the clothes for her.

“Then what about us… Can you guys lend us some clothes too?”

The two women stood to the side, acting delicate.

Yin Qing suddenly had an idea. He glanced at the elder surnamed Lu, then stood up and walked over. Seeing that Lei Yusheng and Lin Xinjie also wanted to stand up, he turned around and spoke with a serious look on his face.

“Sit down!”

After saying this, Yin Qing turned back around, suddenly wearing a bright smile, and walked over quickly.

“How about this: I also have a few sets of clothes, I’ll get them for you two girls!”

The two women covered their mouths and smiled.

“Thank you so much, sir!”

“Ah, it’s alright!”

Yin Qing half-knelt, hiding the panicked sweat on his forehead under the guise that it was due to never having interacted with a woman before.

Out of the corner of his eye, he caught a glimpse of a woman squatting beside him and looking at him. Something seemed to move under her skirt, and a faint, foul odor reached his nose once again, making his movements even more impatient.

‘Found it! ’

What Yin Qing turned to was a letter — the very letter that Ji Yuan had given to Yin Zhaoxian when he first left Ning’an County. When Yin Zhaoxian had gone to Wan Province, he had passed it on to Yin Qing. In addition to being a request that his son use it to practice his handwriting, it was also a kind of encouragement.

Yin Qing quietly took out the letter out of the old envelope, then hid it in his clothes, and took them all out.

“Is this robe alright?”

“As long as we’re not naked, it’s fine~”

A woman joked. She picked up the clothes and shook them out, and a piece of letter paper fell from the cloth.

Swish…

A ray of divine light containing a repelling aura flashed across the letter.

“Ah…”

“Ah…”

“Ah…”

The three women were suddenly so frightened that they leapt away from the campfire, their faces full of fear.

“Oops, Mr. Ji’s letter fell out. It’s my fault for not putting it away properly!”

Yin Qing’s voice was filled with surprise. After confirming that the letter was indeed useful on this occasion, he picked up the paper that had fallen to the ground. In his eyes, each of the words inscribed on it seemed to glow with a faint light.

Although the letter written by Ji Yuan long ago was not an Edict, the will and spirit it contained at the time had remained condensed in it for many years.

人 (rén): person, 老成 (lǎochéng): mature/experienced, 精 (jīng): essence/proficient/etc. Lit. “experienced people are proficient/skilled”

书呆子 (shūdāizi): lit. bookish fool

小心驶得万年船 Lit. “when set said, be sure to obtain a ten-thousand-year-old/very-very-old ship” with the implication being that it was good for 10000 years, it’ll be good for a few more

(jìnzhūzhěchì, jìnmòzhěhēi): those who handle cinnabar are stained red; those who work with ink are stained black. Idiom — you are the product of your environment. Could also be translated to “Birds of a feather flock together”

Chapter 218: Song of the Hand-Forged Blade

[T/n: note on chapter title]1

This piece of letter paper would usually be regarded as a treasure worthy of a collection, not because it was a piece of exquisite calligraphy, but because the person who wrote it was no ordinary individual.

But either way, it was just a piece of letter paper after all, and Yin Qing was not sure how much use it would be. But looking at the expressions of these three women, they were startled, at the very least.

“Brother Yin, why is the paper in your hand glowing?”

Mo Xiu was nearest to him, and was extremely surprised when he saw the light shining from the letter paper in Yin Qing’s hand. The traveling merchants and the other two scholars were further away, so they couldn’t see it clearly, but they could still vaguely make out that the piece of paper in Yin Qing’s hands was indeed glowing.

“Hey, you three girls, what’s wrong?”

Yin Qing asked in surprise. Mo Xiu turned to them and chimed in.

“Yeah, what’s wrong?”

“Ah, perhaps we just disturbed them over here. Girls, feel free to take the clothes from the book packs. Brother Mo and I will go over there and make sure not to look at you!”

Yin Qing first placed the letter on the table where Lei Yusheng had slept, then took out the two sets of clothes from his book pack, placing Mo Xiu’s clothes directly on the table. Then he picked up both packs and handed them to Mo Xiu. After retrieving the letter, he also picked up the other packs, in order to avoid them being “accidentally” knocked over again.

“Brother Mo, let’s go.”

Yin Qing gave Mo Xiu a wink, and the latter came back to his senses and returned to the traveling merchants’ camp with Yin Qing, carrying the book packs.

Yin Qing’s original intention had been to come and bring Mo Xiu back, so now was not the time to be brave.

Everyone over there looked at the three women, then many of them focused on the letter in Yin Qing’s hand.

Now that he had gotten closer, the merchants, Lin Xinjie, and Lei Yusheng were finally able to clearly see the paper in Yin Qing’s hand.

Yin Qing didn’t say much. He just put down the book packs alongside Mo Xiu, and then sat in his spot, looking at the letter in his hand.

After what had happened just now, the atmosphere in the deserted waystation became subtle. All of the traveling merchants held their axes tightly in their hands. The four scholars also noticed something wrong with Yin Qing’s frequent glances.

The three women who still appeared to be dripping wet stood ten feet away from the scholar’s fire, always staring at the corner where the men were seated. Because they were a little far from the fire, no one could see the expressions on their faces.

“Whoooo… Whooooo…”

“Boom…”

Outside, the constant noise of the storming wind and rain was interspersed with the occasional sound of thunder. The two campfires inside the waystation occasionally flickered in a gust of wind that leaked in.

After having taken out the letter, Yin Qing’s energy and the determination of the surrounding people seemed to have been increased, and they gathered around as if banding together in hatred over a common enemy. The corner of the deserted inn was filled with rage.

Although the three women couldn’t see it very clearly, they could feel a strong smell of Yang from that corner, which made them feel a little uncomfortable.

After more than a dozen breaths, the three women over there, although afraid, still asked a question; after all, they hadn’t suffered any harm just now.

“Young master~! What is that in your hand? It looks like a treasure!”

Yin Qing was actually still nervous. After all, Mr. Ji’s letter was no substitute for Mr. Ji himself, so it might not be able to save everyone. He glanced at the people around him, calmed himself down, and concentrated on answering the woman’s question.

“It’s nothing special. This is just a letter from an elder. I was careless, and didn’t put it away properly after reading it.”

Yin Qing shook the letter paper, and the light on it seemed to sway like ripples, making it appear very magical.

“You three girls got soaked in the rain. You’d better warm yourself by the fire and change into dry clothes. Otherwise, you will get sick. When your clothes are dry tomorrow, you can change back out of ours. No need to repay us for anything.”

What Yin Qing meant was that he hoped everyone could live in peace and not interfere with one another.

The three women glanced at each other, and finally walked to the fire. They squatted down and hugged their legs for a while, then began to look at the clothes left by the scholars.

“They’re really full of scholarly fragrance. Sisters, since the young master asked us to change our clothes, let’s change them to avoid catching a cold.”

The other two heard her words and laughed. Then they actually stood up together and started to undress.

Originally, there was a table blocking the fire. The corners of these tables in the outpost were not too high, but the tables weren’t all that small, either, If the women truly wanted to change their clothes, they could just squat down, and it would serve as a barrier. However, they didn’t expect that these three people would stand up on purpose, showing off their graceful upper bodies.

As the clothes peeled off, revealing the white skin and red crevices, all the people in the corner stared, their eyes wide open. They knew something was wrong, but weren’t able to look away. Even Yin Qing’s face was agitated. He was naturally curious about women, but he was not so extreme that he would look at them without their permission. However, in this situation where he had to constantly pay attention to the other party’s actions at all times, he could not relax his observation so easily.

“Hmph, why are you pretending to be so serious…”

A woman murmured softly, and uttered a quiet exclamation.

“Hey, sister, it’s all wet inside too~”

“Then just take it off, hee hee…”

This kind of playful scene seems to have become dreamy and even more alluring. Many men felt their mouths dry and wanted to gulp.

The women gave each other narrow glances. They could feel the Yang energy had obviously greatly dissipated, and it was no longer so uncomfortable for them.

Yin Qing himself was fine, but he didn’t notice the changes in the people behind him.

“It’s vulgar!”

The elder surnamed Lu suddenly scolded in a low voice, and knocked the ground with his hatched a few times, making it clang. The letter paper seemed to flash twice as the old man cursed, and many people in the group suddenly sobered.

The result of Old Lu’s scolding and actions obviously angered the three women. They all looked at him coldly, and teeth flashed at the corners of their mouths.

When the three women bared their teeth in anger, Yin Qing suddenly saw an illusion of a long shadow of a mouth and nose. At that moment, he even felt that it seemed a bit familiar.

‘Fox spirits!’

This thought suddenly flashed through Yin Qing’s mind, and the hairs on his body stood up on end. Regardless of exactly what had happened just now, although it was not confirmed, he was now certain.

Combined with what Yin Qing had accidentally glimpsed moving under the woman’s skirt before, it was likely that there would be a fox tail on the lower body that was being blocked by the table.

When the three women stared at Old Man Lu with cold eyes, Yin Qing discovered that the letter paper in his hand seemed to have a faint light emanating from it, and floating around his group of people. Then, many people suddenly could vaguely see that the three women, whose backs were now to the fire, had a faint green glow in their eyes.2

“Oh my god!”3

“The eyes are glowing green!”

“Yeah!”

“Ah, did you see it too? It shone for me too!”

“No, no, I saw it too!”…

By now, everyone had truly been scared awake, with goosebumps all over their bodies.

When the group of women heard what these people were talking about, they were shocked. They had no idea how they had been exposed. However, they realized that their seductive behavior might now have little effect.

The three women no longer flirted or made suggestive gestures. Instead, they narrowed their eyes and looked coldly in the direction of the crowd. The individuals that stuck out the most were naturally the elder surnamed Lu and Yin Qing, and the thing they feared the most was the letter paper in Yin Qing’s hand.

However, after so much time had passed, that piece of paper seemed to have no other effect aside from looking extraordinary. The three women’s apprehension weakened, and they exchanged several glances.

One of the women asked.

“Young man, seeing that you are such an upright person, your family’s teachings must be very strict, right? Your parents and elders are really good at writing!”

Another immediately followed up in a teasing tone.

“It seems like you haven’t tasted the joy of lovemaking yet~”

“Well, the elders in your family must have never taught you this kind of knowledge!”

“Would you like to try it? Ahahahaha…”

The three women swayed back and forth with laughter, but Yin Qing pretended to be indifferent and unmoved. The mysterious aura that circulated around his body became more active, though the letter paper appeared unchanged. He could even vaguely see the woman’s shadow beside the fire opposite occasionally flash with a tail.

He could also see waves of faint smoke-like substances rising from the woman’s body. They grew thicker and thicker as the rumbling thunder outside grew louder.

Before, only Yin Qing could smell the slight stench, but now, before they realized, everyone could now make it out. Combined with the scene they saw before, the three women now gave them hardly any temptation whatsoever.

The women’s words were unbearable, but they were actually testing him. They even moved their bodies and slowly approached the other campfire. Although their appearances had not changed, they became increasingly creepy to everyone’s senses!

“Boom…” The thunder sounded again.

A sense of crisis grew stronger in Yin Qing’s heart, and he realized that these three vixen spirits were not going to give up.

“Uncle Lu, do you traveling merchants have any traditional methods?”

Yin Qing asked in a low voice, without looking back to face him. Although Mr. Ji’s letter was miraculous, it was just a letter, after all.

Elder Lu and others were also more fearful now, but after hearing Yin Qing’s words, they remembered the method passed down from generation to generation in their village. After humming a bit in reflection, Elder Lu spoke to everyone.

“Everyone, prepare the Song of the Hand-Forged Blade.”

The group of merchants looked at each other and grabbed their hatchets.

“Clang…clang…”

“Clang…clang…”

Over a dozen people began to hit the ground with the backs of their blades. In the span of a few breaths, the sounds went from haphazard to more and more orderly.

“Clang clang clang… Clang clang clang…”

“Merchants climbing the mountains, hey-oh… A bandit blocking the road—hey-oh… With a blade in hand I go—hey-oh…”

“Clang clang clang… Clang clang clang…”

“With a huge load on my back—hey-oh… Through mountains and wild outback—hey-oh… I fear no beast nor foe—hey-oh…”4

“Clang clang clang… Clang clang clang…”

As the dozen or so merchants sang louder and louder, their aura became more and more powerful. Yin Qing found that the light from the letter in his hand had also become much stronger, and there was now a thin glow of light that flowed around the dozen or so merchants. A murderous aura rose from this group of seemingly simple men.

The suffocating Yang energy became stronger and stronger, causing the expressions of the three women to change, and forcing them to take several steps back. These were obviously just mortals, but they seemed too ferocious, as if they were about to chop people in half with their blades.

T/n: you know, if you think about it, Yin Qing got the letter paper from his dad. What kind of mystical encounters must his dad have had off camera that the letter saved him from to make him want to pass it down to his son?

Also, to the fox spirits: just move to the modern era, there are a truly ridiculous number of people who would cheerfully give their lifespans for a chance with a fox girl

土法 (tǔfǎ): “traditional method,” 刀子 (dāozi): knife, 歌 (gē): song

Foxes, cats, etc. have a layer at the back of their eyes called the tapetum lucidum, which serves to reflect light back through the retina and gives them a higher sensitivity to light in the dark, hence their night vision. This layer also reflects light shined on it, and the eyes glow green. Humans don’t have this.

Lit. “oh mommy!”

Chapter 219: Humans Are More Vicious Than Monsters

Yin Qing vaguely understood that this letter could boost his state of mind, provided that his state of mind remained steady. It would not work if he were in a situation where the majority of the people around him were afraid, like they had been just now.

But Yin Qing was still rather anxious, because the song would eventually end. Although the traveling merchants had a strong momentum now, and the three monsters were a little scared, what would happen later?

“Hoooo…”

“Hiss…”

At this stage, the three women no longer tried to disguise themselves. Each of them bared their teeth and made threatening sounds, like wild beasts. With the help of the letter’s spiritual aura, everyone could even vaguely see fur on their faces.

Although this situation was frightening, it also made the merchants strike the ground with their hatchets even more frequently.

At the end of the song, all that could be heard was the dense “clang, clang clang…” sounds of the axes.

Yin Qing felt that something was wrong. He was about to say something, when he found that all the merchants had stood up.

“Pfft…”

Elder Lu spat on each of his hands.

“Pft…”

“Pft…”

“Pft…”

…

The other traveling merchants all spat on their hands as well, then rubbed them together, and tightly gripped the handles of their axes and machetes.

“Afraid of shit, huh?!”1

“F**k you!”

“Who’s the bandit now, huh?!”2

“That’s right!”

…

The mob of merchants cursed with all kinds of dirty language and stalked forwards, blades in hand. It turned out that they were not bluffing. Yin Qing and his three classmates were all stunned.

There was a saying among this generation of traveling merchants in Lishun Prefecture: “Sing the Blade Song for strength, then pick up your blade when you’re done.”3

This was a group of people who traveled all over the poor mountains and muddy rivers with their goods on their backs just to scrounge for food. There could be as few as a dozen individuals in a group, or as many as several dozens or even a hundred. When cornered or provoked, they truly did dare to fight against bandits and criminals with just their hatchets.

Moreover, most of the traveling merchants had family inheritances of skills, shortcuts, or some knowledge. Some might even have some basic martial arts skills or some folk methods to ward off evil spirits, all passed down from the elders to the younger generation.

Under normal circumstances, even if there were strong men blocking the road, many of the latter would choose to retreat upon seeing the traveling merchants pounding at the rocks on the ground and singing the Blade Song.

To be honest, if the merchants chose not to do business, they could definitely just become bandits anywhere they wanted, which was definitely better than ordinary farmers, who could not survive in that life.

Yin Qing discovered that the spiritual light continued to flow out of the letter in his hand, and the fierce aura of the traveling merchants became more and more intense. By the light of the campfire, the merchants’ shadows even seemed to be emitting a faint smoke-like substance.

“These three women4 don’t look human!”

“But they don’t look like ghosts either!”

“You’ll know what it is once you chop it up!”

“Hah!”

“Go!”

“Kill them!”

Having just seen these three women with green eyes and hair on their faces, no one still believed that they were human whatsoever. At this moment, all of the merchants were extremely enthusiastic, and their earlier fear was pushed to the back of their minds.

With a fierce murderous aura, they rushed towards the three women.

Not only were Yin Qing and others stunned to see this scene, but it was also apparently the first time that these three fox spirits had encountered such a situation. They were so frightened by the murderous aura that they dodged to the sides.

As soon as they showed weakness, the merchants became even more aggressive.

“They’re afraid of us!”

“Hahahaha, they’re afraid of us!”

Three or four men mercilessly swung their blades together at a woman in a green silk skirt, going straight for her head and torso.

“Aaaah…”

The woman screamed and dodged. The situation was the same for the other two; they were forced to flee. Under this powerful and ferocious aura, even their seductive techniques had no effect.

These fox spirits were mainly proficient in illusion and charm techniques. If these methods failed, the fox spirits would be in a state of panic. This group of men had no other ideas; they just wanted to chop and kill the monsters.

At that moment, Yin Qing couldn’t help but be reminded of Hu Yun acting cowardly in the face of dogs. He shouted out a warning.

“Those three are fox spirits, I just saw their tails!”

“So it’s a vixen, no wonder it was so flirty!”

“She also stinks like a fox!”

“Hahaha! It’s just the right time of year to kill a fox and skin it for merchandise.”

“Come and die!”

The last exclamation was from a big man wielding both a hatchet and an axe with murderous intent, forcing the three women into a corner without any extra words. It wasn’t sophisticated martial arts, just haphazard strikes. He couldn’t reach the nimble women, but it looked scary.

Often, when one of the traveling merchants was kicked or hit, feeling the pain and being pushed back, someone else would step in and strike them with their blade. Some didn’t even care and would even let themselves be hit, because their opponents looked weak either way, with thin arms and legs.

“Riiip…”

Someone slashed one of the thin skirts with a knife and saw a big tail.

“It’s a tail, the fox’s tail is out!”

With this kind of random slashing, just as someone was about to hit the woman, he found that her clothes had suddenly deflated, and a fox leapt out from her skirt. The other two women also revealed their true forms.

“It’s a fox!”

“Catch them!”

The three foxes jumped around with extreme dexterity, scratching several people with their claws and biting them with their fangs.

One of them ran to Old Lu and opened its mouth wide to bite his neck, but Elder Lu grabbed it by the tail and slammed it to the ground.

“Bang!”

“Wuwuwuwu…”

The fox stood up and grabbed Old Lu’s wrist, but despite the pain he didn’t let go, and became even more ferocious.

“If you dare to grab me, then you will die by my hand!”

Old Lu had a glare on his face, but he didn’t even have time to swing his knife.

“Pffrrt…”

“Pffrrt…”

The foxes all farted, and a stench filled the air.5

“Whoooo, whooo, whoooo……”

“Whoooo, whooo, whoooo……”

After farting, the three foxes hurriedly rushed to the corner of the outer wall of the deserted waystation in a hurry, crawling through rotting gap in the ground.

“Cough cough… It smells so bad!”

“It stinks…”

“Cough cough…”

“Blarrrrggg…”6

“The fumes hurt my eyes! Blarrrrggg…”

“Open the door, open the door… Blarrrrggg…”

“Ugh, cough, cough…”

This stinky fart was so pungent that it made people dizzy.

A group of frustrated merchants covered their noses and chased each other to the door, opened it, and looked outside.

“Boom…”

There was thunder and lightning outside, along with strong wind and heavy rain.

“Bah…”

“Let them run away.”

“Haah…haah…haaah…”

“Damn, it smells so f***ing bad!”

“Hiss… I was bitten just now, it hurts so much. Hooh… hooh… I almost died from the stench.”

“I was also scratched several times, and these wounds aren’t shallow…”

“Let me get some fresh air to breathe.”

“Damn, it stinks!”

“Pah…”

A group of men spat and cursed at the door for a while. After a while, the stench dissipated, and they finally closed the door, moving a table over to block the small hole in it

When everyone returned to the campfire, the excitement they had felt just now still had not yet subsided.

“Damn, we almost killed the monsters.”

“Exactly, I almost caught one.”

“Hahaha, they were nothing compared to bandits!”

“Uncle Lu even caught one, but it ran away before he could kill it.”

“Wouldn’t a monster’s pelt be even more valuable?”

“It’s a pity that we didn’t kill any!”

“Exactly.”

“Alright, alright, let’s take care of the wounds.”

“This damn beast! It smells so foul, and its bite hurts so much.”

The merchants were in high spirits. They chatted enthusiastically, mixing in cries of pain and curses as they rummaged through their packs for herbs, liquor, and other items.

Yin Qing and his three companions huddled in the corner like four quails. They did their best to speak as little as possible, and tried not to interfere with the merchants’ actions. They didn’t even dare to run from the stench just now. The ferocity of this group of people was truly terrifying. They even felt that they were no less vicious than the monsters.

“Young man, thank you for what you did just now!”

The leader of the team, the man surnamed Lu, bandaged the wound on his hand, then came over to thank Yin Qing. He looked at the letter in his hand; there was no longer any light on it, and it looked like an ordinary letter from home.

Yin Qing hurriedly stood up and bowed with cupped hands.

“We should be the ones thanking you. If you hadn’t taken up your swords and stood up, we would have been in great danger tonight.”

“Yes, yes, thank you to all of you heroes!”

Hearing that Lei Yusheng even called them “heroes,” the men nearby were quite happy.

“Hahahaha, we must have scared the scholars.”

“Scholar Yin, you were great, too!”

“Not bad, not bad. Scholars are different, after all. The cleverness he showed just now is incomparable to ours.”

“Come on, we have wine and broth here, come have a drink!”

“That’s right, you’re welcome here. I’m not stupid: I know that the talisman in Scholar Yin’s hand was of great help just now.”

“What do you know? That’s a letter.”

Because of this incident, the merchants changed their previous indifferent attitude towards the scholars and became extremely enthusiastic. After having being afraid for some time, the group of scholars also changed their impression of the merchants in turn. The distance between the two groups in the deserted waystation was quickly eliminated, and the atmosphere became warm.

Some were chatting about the fox spirits they had just defeated, some asked if Yin Qing’s letter had been blessed, some gossiped about family matters, and some even discussed the fox spirits’ figures and said some obscene words.

However, in the midst of the stormy night, on a hill a little further away, three foxes drenched in the heavy rain stared in the direction of the deserted waystation with ferocious looks on their faces.

怕个鸟啊 Lit. “afraid of a bird, huh!” with “bird” as a euphemism — like, “I ain’t afraid of shit”

比 (bǐ): to compare, 山贼 (shānzéi): brigand, 又 (yòu): once again/and yet, 怎么样 (zěnmeyàng): how about it? 嘛 (ma): indicates that the speaker thinks something is obvious/pause for emphasis — literally “how do we compare to brigands, huh?”

The exact words used are 个 (gè): classifier for individual, 娘 (niáng): effeminate/lady, 们 (men): marker for nouns referring to individuals. Essentially means “this female-looking thing”

Chapter 220: Three Pecks

“Crashshshshshsh… Crashshshshsh…”

The heavy rain showed no signs of easing throughout the night. in the middle of the night. The hill where the three foxes were located was about a mile away from the deserted waystation.

Although the rain affected visibility, with the three vixen’s eyesight, they could still vaguely make out some firelight and smoke coming from the barren outpost.

“I’m so angry! I’m so mad! We were driven away by a group of mortals!”

“Oooh…that damn old man grabbed my tail, he almost, almost… That scared me to death…”

The fox on the left still had lingering fears. Old Lu had almost hit it with his knife. Judging from the murderous intent of the traveling merchants just now, if it had been a hit, he would have struck to kill.

“Hmph, I blame you two cowards. They’re just a bunch of mortals. They might be a little fierce, but so what? With your fox body, you can easily bite them to death, but you panicked. I really…”

“Boom…crack!”

Lightning struck a large tree by the hill.

“Ah…”

“Ah…”

“Ah…”

The three foxes’ fur stood on end, and they screamed and fled in three different directions.

Inside the barren waystation, everyone was tired after their previous fight. However, they could not relax their vigilance now, so the number of people on night watch significantly increased.

Half of the traveling merchants went on night watch, while the other half rested. Meanwhile, Yin Qing and his friends took turns sleeping in pairs. Although the merchants said that they could sleep freely and that they would keep an eye on them, the scholars had grown a bit more mature, after all, and knew that one should always be on guard.

When Lei Yusheng woke up early in the morning, the sound of rain outside had stopped.

“Are you awake?”

Yin Qing rubbed his eyes, yawned, and asked. Then, he stood up, stretching his arms and legs. Lin Xinjie, who was sitting beside him, was still sleeping soundly on the low table.

Lei Yusheng looked at Mo Xiu next to him. Yin Qing and Lin Xinjie were supposed to wake him and Mo Xiu up in the latter half of the night to replace them on watch, but based on the current situation, Yin Qing and Lin Xinjie had to stand guard all night; or, perhaps it would be more accurate to say that Yin Qing was the only one keeping watch for most of the night.

“Yin Qing, you didn’t sleep?”

Yin Qing stretched out his arms and turned back with a smile.

“I’m in better spirits, so it’s okay for me to sleep less.”

“That’s right, this Young Master Yin also read books to us at night, telling us about the customs and practices of other regions. He is in good spirits, and his knowledge is also outstanding!”

“Indeed, he is quite knowledgeable.”

“Young and promising.”

One merchant who had already gotten up informed Lei Yusheng cheerfully, and the people around him echoed his words.

As more people got up, the noise in the outpost grew louder. Lin Xinjie and Mo Xiu were also woken up, and the whole deserted waystation became lively once again.

Old Boss Lu picked out some thin pieces of firewood, threw them into the charcoal pile, blew on it a few times, and rekindled the campfire with the remaining charcoal. Then he set up a pot and began to prepare breakfast.

Everyone including the four scholars was given a bowl of corn porridge, with a steamed bun heated by the fire and a small spoonful of pickled vegetables.

Even Lei Yusheng and Lin Xinjie, who were used to fine food, ate it with relish and praised the merchants for their good cooking skills.

After everyone had packed up, the group of nearly twenty people finally hoisted their bags and book packs and walked out of the deserted outpost.

After the rain, the mountains seemed to have been purified. The air was fresh and the sun was bright. Just taking a few deep breaths outside could make one feel refreshed.

“Let’s go, let’s hurry up. It will only take two more days to cross Datong Mountain. Will you scholars also come with us?”

Yin Qing and the others looked at each other, and Mo Xiu asked straightforwardly.

“Two days? I heard that it takes seven to eight days.”

“Hahaha, that’s along the postal road, which twists and turns along the mountainside. We have our own familiar roads. They’re a little harder to walk, but much faster. In the early years, many of Wan Province’s silk fabrics were transported on the backs of people like us. There was no road back then, after all!”

They had already encountered fox spirits on this mountain, so Yin Qing and others did not dare to continue on by themselves. When they heard Old Boss Lu’s suggestion, they naturally agreed immediately.

The group walked along the postal road for about half an hour. After that, they no longer followed the winding path, but traveled directly to the top of the mountain, and, following Old Boss Lu’s lead, turned off in another direction.

To be honest, aside from Yin Qing, the other three scholars seemed to be struggling and slowing them down, even Mo Xiu, who was not from a wealthy family.

So later on, the merchants simply traded out the contents of their book packs for a few of the lighter items and placed the boxes on their backs, then helped and supported them onwards from time to time, which allowed the scholars to persevere.

Only Yin Qing did not ask for help from beginning to end, and was able to maintain a good physical strength to keep up with the pace of the traveling merchants, making the other men look at him with even more admiration.

“Haah… haaah… haah… We finally made it to Huanggou Ridge!”1

After climbing over the mountain, the group’s leader, Old Boss Lu, was also out of breath. Because it had rained the night before, the air was fresh, but the ground was also muddy. Even if they found places with more rocks to step on, it still consumed more energy than normal.

Looking at the ones behind him, they all showed signs of fatigue, and the couple of scholars were huffing and panting like bellows with punctured holes.

“Everyone, let’s take a rest. No one is allowed to go too far. Even if you need to relieve yourself, find someone to be with you!”

“Okay.”

“Oh, a break.”

“Finally, we get a break…”

“I’m going to fall apart…”

Some people joked while others complained, and they all found a dry spot to sit down nearby.

A crow flew by overhead, giving a series of caws.

Lin Xinjie murmured subconsciously.

“Mr. Lu, isn’t the crow’s cry unlucky?”

A man who was drinking water next to him was amused at his words.

“Hahahaha… You, scholar, aren’t most of you not superstitious? There are so many birds and beasts in the mountains. If you get scared hearing a crow, you’ll be trembling all day; why don’t you leave? I’ve even heard a King of Beasts2 roar!”

“In other words…”

“This scholar…”

Hearing others teasing him, Yin Qing perked up.

“Uncle, have you truly heard a tiger roar? What kind of roar was it? Were you scared at the time? I have a friend who often hears tigers roar, and he says he is scared to death every time. Is that true?”

A few of the traveling merchants nearby looked at Yin Qing strangely. The man who was being asked also asked a question back out of curiosity.

“Your friend who often hears tigers roar, is he alive or dead? He must be really brave!”

“Hahahaha, you say he’s brave? He’s the most cowardly guy I know!”

Thinking of Hu Yun’s cowardly behavior, Yin Qing couldn’t help but laugh. The Old Turtle told him stories before about how the fox had looked when he was frightened by him.

“My friend said that the tiger’s roar was so loud that it could be heard from ten li away, and all the birds and beasts of the mountains fled. Uncle, tell me about the tiger roar you heard!”

When the others heard Yin Qing’s words, they thought that this friend must have been bragging.

“The roar of a tiger is indeed scary, but it’s not that exaggerated. With a dozen or so of us traveling merchants together, even if we meet the King of Beasts, the creature probably won’t dare to harm us. The Blade Song is not just sung.”

“Yes, in fact, we heard a King of Beasts roar at that time, and went down the mountain to tell the hunters. The next day, more than 20 hunters came up the mountain with their tools to lay traps.”

Mo Xiu said dazedly, “You’re willing to sacrifice your lives for the sake of a hide?”

Old Boss Lu smiled and shook his head.

“At that time, the King of Beasts must have wandered over from other mountain areas. We couldn’t help it because it was too close to the other villages over there. It’s not that the hunters want the hide, but rather that their entire families, young and old, all live at the foot of the mountain, so they were worried!”

“Did you catch the King of Beasts in the end?”

Lei Yusheng was also curious.

“We had already left the region by then, and we only came back several months later. It was said that they still hadn’t caught the King of Beasts. Finally, several villages around there raised money to build a Land God Temple, and went to the City God Temple to pray to the City God. The old temple priest helped preside over the ritual and invited a Land God to protect them. Let me tell you, it really worked…”

This was also an interesting story. The scholars listened to it with great interest. For a couple of young people who had just joined the traveling merchant group, this was also their first time hearing it, too.

Yin Qing was listening when he suddenly felt an itch on his neck. He turned his head and found a paper crane had landed on his shoulder, and was pecking at him with its small paper beak.

Yin Qing’s expression was filled with joy at the sight of the paper crane. He stretched out his hand to cover his shoulder, turned his head, and and lowered his voice.

“Little Paper Crane, is Mr. Ji here?”

After thinking for a while, he added.

“Peck once for yes, twice for no.”

The paper crane tilted its head to look at Yin Qing, then pecked the hand that was covering it three times.

Yin Qing frowned, then asked tentatively.

“He came here, then left again?”

This time the paper crane pecked once, then flapped its wings and flew behind a bush to one side. Yin Qing saw it leave and quickly followed.

“Hey Yin Qing, where are you going?”

“To relieve myself!”

“Then I’ll go with you!”

Mo Xiu was listening to the traveling merchant’s storytelling. When he saw Yin Qing running away alone, he quickly followed. However, Yin Qing ran too fast, and he obviously lagged behind by a large distance.

Of course, Yin Qing didn’t care that Mo Xiu was following behind him. He kept chasing the paper crane around several bushes, then turned to the side of the mountain wall. Suddenly, he saw something behind him, and let out a frightened cry: “Ah!”

A hairy, squat, hunch-backed monster with a tan body and wearing a tattered coat stood there, the paper crane circling above its head.

“I-I great Y-Young Master Yin! I-I am-m the Mountain God of of Datong Mountain, I’m Zun, Immortal Zun…”

“Yin Qing~!”

Mo Xiu’s voice rang out. Yin Qing looked back, and when he turned back around, the monster claiming to be a Mountain God had disappeared.

荒 (huāng): desolate/to neglect, 沟 (gōu): ditch/ravine/gully — “Desolate Ditch Ridge” — a charming name, truly

Chapter 221: Pay Respect to the Mountain

“Yin Qing, why did you run so far to relieve yourself? This mountain is dangerous!”

Mo Xiu came over, panting slightly, only to see Yin Qing standing there stupidly.

“Are you already done? I might as well go then, too.”

While speaking, Mo Xiu went behind a rock and started to unfasten his belt.

“No, no, I haven’t started peeing yet.”

Yin Qing responded and hurriedly approached the other side of the mountain face. While unfastening his pants, he looked around. He did not see the strange-looking man who claimed to be a mountain god with a stutter, nor did he see the paper crane.

In truth, Yin Qing had only seen the paper crane two or three times before, and each time he watched the paper crane fly to Hu Yun to peck at him. He had heard about how Mr. Ji wrote a lot of words on the paper crane, but the surface of its body still looked white and empty.

By the time Yin Qing and Mo Xiu had both finished urinating, Yin Qing had inspected almost every stone and bush with his gaze, but still couldn’t find anything.

“Let’s go, what are you looking at?”

“Ah, let’s go.”

There was nothing else he could do, so Yin Qing had to go back with Mo Xiu. However, on the way back, the paper crane flew back to Yin Qing’s side once more, sliding into his arms while Yin Qing was surprised.

In fact, the paper crane was not very intelligent. In addition to its instinct to seek good fortune and avoid danger, it could also simply distinguish its master’s orders and give clear responses. The reason why it had pecked Yin Qing three times earlier was not because it understood the true meaning of the question, but because it understood basic facts: one peck meant that its master was here, two pecks meant he wasn’t here anymore.

When Yin Qing and Mo Xiu returned to where everyone was resting, the group was preparing to set off again. They recounted some interesting tales in the meantime, and had been resting for a quarter of an hour.

The group took another half hour to circumnavigate Huanggou Ridge. The terrain began to descend from its previous upward trend, until they finally reached a large downhill sloping path.

“Great! Finally, I don’t have to climb up anymore. I can finally walk down. This will save a lot of energy!”

Lin Xinjie was so excited that he announced these words as if he had been given an imperial pardon.

“Haha, young man, there is a saying that it is easier to go up the mountain than to go down. This section of the road is where you need to be careful. Huzi, Li Yin, Chuanzi, keep an eye on these three scholars, make sure they avoid any accidents.”

“Hey!”

“I understand, Uncle Lu.”

“I understand, Uncle Lu.”

The three scholars scratched their heads, but didn’t ask why Yin Qing was not included in the protection. After all, as long as they weren’t blind, they could see that Yin Qing’s physical strength was not inferior to that of the traveling merchants.

Sure enough, just as Old Boss Lu had said, going down was far more difficult than going up. It consumed a lot of physical energy. Some people felt that their legs were going to give out as they walked down the mountain. In addition, the road was slippery after the rain, so everyone advanced extremely carefully.

A strong man whose leg was bitten previously was full of energy, and didn’t feel that it was expending too much of his energy. However, on his very next step, he landed on a loose stone.

“Hey, hey…”

“Be careful!”

“Grab him! Grab him!”

“Clatter-taktaktak…”

The stone rolled directly down the mountain path, and the man was so frightened that he couldn’t stop slipping, even after planting his feet. He was about to fall all the way down, but he suddenly found several small protrusions on the mountain path under his feet, which gave him a foothold. Only then was he able to stabilize his toppling body and grab a small tree nearby.

“Are you okay, Xiao Liu?”

“Brother Liu!”

“Slow down, take it easy!”

A few people to the side quickly gathered around.

“Hooh… That was dangerous, I almost fell. If I had rolled all the way down like that, I might have lost my life!”

The man surnamed Liu sat down on the slope, looked down. and wiped the cold sweat off his forehead with his sleeves. He had sweated more in that moment than he had in the entire previous half hour.

“Hey, Brother Liu, there’s blood on your trouser legs!”

Hearing this, the man rolled up his trouser leg and looked at the place where he had been bitten by the fox yesterday. Sure enough, blood started seeping through the bandage. He untied it to take a look and found that the wound had split open again.

“Ouch, it looks a little infected. This fox’s mouth is quite poisonous!”

“Let’s watch over it tonight. Now is not the time to rest!”

After being frightened by this incident, the group traveled more cautiously. At noon, they had a meal of cold water in bamboo tubes, some biscuits, and steamed buns. Before dark, the merchants found a cave on the trail that was about one zhang deep and about four or five zhang wide.1

This cave looked quite scary, as if a large rock had been split down the middle by some supernatural axe, and felt as though it would fall down at any time. However, it was in fact very stable. This was a customary place for this group of traveling merchants to take shelter and rest.

Some people were busy cooking, while many others were unbuttoning their clothes and trousers to check the places where they had been bitten and scratched last night.

“Hooh…”

“Wow… this is serious!”

“It’s all infected…”

“My skin is all swollen, why didn’t I feel any pain or itching during the day?”

“Ouch, now that you mentioned itching, I feel a little itchy now.”

“Don’t scratch it!”

“Treat it again with herbs!”

Everyone was worried now. After all, they had not been injured by an ordinary fox. The wound’s progress was not looking good. However, they were lucky enough to find a lot of herbs along the way. Now, several people were making poultices and preparing to apply medicine to everyone.

Behind the bushes in the distance, three foxes gazed at the group of mortals in the distance with cold eyes.

“Hehehehe…”

“If you dare to fight against this old lady, you must not even know how to write the word ‘death’!”2

“We must devour these people!”

“Aiya, those scholars… Let’s slow down first, maybe spend a night of passion together first~”

“Yes, yes, hee hee hee…”

They had not truly cultivated the ability to transform into a human form. Their human appearance was just an illusion, similar to an inflatable balloon, and could be easily popped. They were essentially incapable of engaging in sexual acts with humans. Their so-called ‘night of passion’ was nothing more than seducing a scholar and sucking away his essence when he was in an excited state.

On the other side, the merchants and scholars under the monsters’ malicious gazes felt some uneasiness in their hearts. They simply gathered around the campfire, and did not dare to go too far, even to pick up firewood.

“Everyone, be careful. There are no walls to protect us tonight. On top of wild beasts, who knows, maybe those three vixens from last night will come back!”

After so many years as a traveling merchant, he had encountered numerous strange and bizarre events. However, most of them were either not worth investigating, or he didn’t want to investigate them too deeply. In order to avoid trouble, he would rather keep things unclear. However, this time was no doubt the most bizarre, as he had encountered a real monster.

“Awooo…”

“Awooooo…”

After night fell, the howling of wolves sounded in the mountains, making everyone more nervous. After a few moments of silence, it sounded again.

“Awooo…”

Many people brought out their hatchets and huddled behind the fire.

“Uncle Lu, the wolf howls seem to be getting closer?”

The man who spoke glanced at the others, and a little sweat beaded on his face.

“Well… we have to be more careful tonight. The hungry wolves might be more dangerous than those vixens. As the old saying goes, a pack of hungry wolves is more ferocious than a tiger…”

However, the three fox spirits they were talking about were clearly even more nervous than the traveling merchants. In a small valley about a hundred feet away from the traveling merchants, the three foxes had already huddled together.

“Hic-wuuu…”

“Wuwuwuwu…”3

“Awoooo…”

A pack of wild mountain wolves appeared nearby, their numbers constantly increasing, surrounding the three foxes from far away. Many of them bared their teeth and roared at the foxes, saliva dripping from their fangs.

“Ah, what’s wrong with these beasts…”

“Why are these beasts coming at us?”

“Bi-big sister, what should we do?”

“There so many wolves, have all the wolf packs in Datong Mountain come here…?”

The eyes the wild wolves glowed green in the moonlight. Some perched on high slopes, while others were on the near side of the mountain. They all stared at the three foxes.

The human beings a hundred or so zhang away started a fire. Although the fire was visible through the many trees and boulders, the wolves obviously ignored the humans’ plans.

As if they had received some kind of signal, one of the leading wolves let out a roar: “AWOOO…” and all the wild wolves bared their ferocious fangs and rushed towards the foxes.

As the saying went, everything had a weakness.4 Although the three fox spirits had refined their horizontal bones and gained spiritual powers, their cultivation path was different; their own cultivation and abilities were not enough to make up for their natural defects. What was more, wolves and even dogs have certain abilities to destroy evil, so it could be said that these foxes had encountered their nemeses.

“Aaaah…”

“Ugh…”

“Wuwuwu…”

“Roooar…”

“Aaaah…

“Roar…”

“Big sister, save me–“

The roars and screams of the vixen and the wolves clearly startled the crowd a little ways away. The roars of the beasts that were fighting were terrifying, and they sounded so close that it even felt like they were just outside the cave.

“Hey… I thought I heard a cry for help just now. Was someone…”

“Stop talking nonsense, I didn’t hear anything!”

“Shh, don’t speak, be careful not to get their attention, let’s increase the fire!”

“Yes, yes, make the fire burn brighter!”

The whole group was trembling with fear. The wild wolves seemed to be going crazy tonight, and there were wolf howls both far away and nearby. The sound of biting and fighting continued outside the cave for a long time, and then even continued on to farther places.

In any case, no one had the courage to go out and check on anything before dawn came.

The next morning, the entire group that hadn’t slept all night dragged their tired bodies and set off cautiously. After hesitating for a long time, they finally moved towards the place where they had first heard the sound of the beasts fighting last night.

When they arrived at the small valley, they saw some traces of tragedy.

“Woah… that’s a lot of blood!”

“Yeah, and here is some blood-stained hair!”

Old Boss Lu walked behind a bush, poked it with a hatchet, and pulled out a piece of red-haired, bloody claw, attached to a dismembered leg.

“This… looks like a fox’s leg?”

“Looks like it… isn’t that…”

“Hooh… could this be from the vixen from the night before last?”

“I really can’t tell!”

“Oh… Datong Mountain is too dangerous!”

Old Boss Lu had a gloomy expression on his face. He wondered whether he should consider taking the merchant routes through Datong Mountain less often in the future, or simply just give up the route entirely.

“Let’s go, hurry up, we must get out of the mountain before it gets dark today, it’s too dangerous here!”

“Yeah! Uncle Lu is right!”

“Hurry up, let’s go!”

No one in the group dared to hesitate any longer. Even the scholars stopped complaining about being tired along the way; it was better to lose a leg than to lose a life.

When the sun began to set that evening, nearly twenty exhausted people finally walked out of the uninhabited boundary line marking the edge of Datong Mountain.

As soon as they turned out of the small road, they saw a large outpost station that was still in operation on the mountainside ahead, and everyone breathed a sigh of relief.

Yin Qing, who felt some movement in his arms, looked down, then turned to look behind him. Behind a large beige-yellow rock beside the mountain path, the hunchbacked Mountain God bowed to him from afar.

Seeing this scene, Yin Qing suddenly understood what happened last night. He suspected that it had been the Mountain God who had taken action, so he hurriedly bowed and paid his respects.

“Scholar Yin, what are you bowing to?”

“I’m thanking the God of the mountain. If there was a god of this mountain, he must have blessed us to get out of the mountain safely this time!”

“Yes, yes, it makes sense!”

“Then I’ll also say goodbye.”

“Me too!”

After experiencing the recent incidents, everyone in the group still had lingering fears, and sincerely went to pay their respects to the Mountain God together.

Rather than being a Mountain God, it would be more accurate to classify it as a mountain spirit who was still cultivating towards the position of Mountain God. At most, it had barely stepped into the threshold of divinity, and did not care much for the beasts in the mountains or the people who travelled through it.

Moreover, there was no Mountain God Temple in Datong Mountain. It wasn’t that the Mountain God didn’t want one, but rather that he didn’t yet have the ability to gather incense power, nor had he grasped the essence of how to do so. But at this moment, the land spirit hiding behind the rocks suddenly felt the power of these mortals’ prayers.

Chapter 222: Corruption in Wan Province

Strictly speaking, the route the traveling merchants took ended at a very different location than where Yin Qing and his companions had originally planned to exit the mountains. It was not possible to cross Datong Mountain diagonally, so they had taken a shortcut and crossed it over a short distance.

But once they reached the other side of the mountain, things became a lot simpler, especially since there were carriages and horses for rent at the large waystation.

Although Yin Qing and the traveling merchants had only known each other for a few days, they had a very good impression of one another. However, they inevitably had to part ways.

Although they had found some new herbs to stabilize their injuries, the traveling merchants still had to go see a doctor. Yin Qing and others also needed to hire a carriage and rush to Lishun Prefecture’s capital city. After saying goodbye, they each left separately. However, the events of the past few days would definitely be unforgettable for all of them.

As they sat in the carriage and advanced along the relatively smooth official road, the four scholars finally felt at ease.

“Haah… If I went back to the academy and told our teachers and other classmates about what happened to us these past few days, do you think they would believe it?”

Lin Xinjie, who was the most talkative and empathetic, sighed with deep emotion in his speech.

“They would… at most just think of it as a story, right?”

Lei Yusheng wasn’t quite sure, while Mo Xiu glanced at them and then at Yin Qing.

“There are fox spirits in Datong Mountain. What will other other travelers do if they encounter them? Some people at the waystation have heard about it before, but it seems that no one intends to do anything about it…”

Before, when they had come out of the mountain, they told the people at the official waystation about their experiences in the mountains. The people there didn’t scoff at them, instead telling them about how other people had also had also experienced similar incidents of being seduced by women in the mountains. After coming out of the mountains, they had been listless, and some even found that people in their team were missing. The people at the local waystation said that they had already reported it to the local government.

But it seemed that would be of no use.

Yin Qing looked at Mo Xiu.

“Well, of course people from the local county government go and take a look, but there is a large difference between human crimes and a monster’s actions, so it may not be that easy to deal with. Instead, we should send official documents to the villages on the mountainside and let those experienced hunters take their mountain dogs to hunt foxes. Who knows, it might work.”

This was the optimal solution that Yin Qing had arrived at. After seeing those fox spirits, and based on what he knew about Hu Yun, he believed that it was as the Old Turtle had said: Dazhen was peaceful, after all, and powerful monsters were rare. The old hunters and their mountain dogs should be able to deal with monsters like these fox spirits.

After finishing his speech, Yin Qing also comforted him.

“Don’t think about it too much. Those foxes might have already been finished. Didn’t you see that bloody fox leg and the signs of a fight? There were quite a few wolves the night before…”

“Oh, that’s right…”

“Well, Brother Yin is right. I, too, think that there may truly be a Mountain God on Datong Mountain.”

“Yeah, this time we really… Oh, and by the way, Yin Qing’s letter, it really glowed that night! Wasn’t it dazzling?”

“Mhm! And the handwriting is really good!”

“Yes, it must be the work of a famous master.”

Seeing that the three of them were talking about the letter again, Yin Qing was irritated as he replied.

“It is often said that scholars should act with integrity and nurture great virtue, so this letter must work with precisely that. As we have righteousness in our hearts, sooner or later, we will be able to protect ourselves.”

Regardless of whether the others believed his words, Yin Qing himself believed in the power of the righteous spirit, even though he knew that this letter did not work quite like that.

. . .

Ji Yuan didn’t go directly to Lishun Prefecture; instead, he rushed to Wan Province before calculating Yin Qing’s approximate location. When he reached the sky above Datong Mountain, he happened to sense the energy of his letter. Ji Yuan knew that Yin Qing was in trouble.

However, when he arrived, the three foxes had been chased and hacked at by the group of traveling merchants, and had escaped the deserted outpost. Ji Yuan found this all quite funny.

In truth, those three fox spirits were indeed insignificant, but they were still monsters that had refined their horizontal bones, after all. To have been chased out like this, even Ji Yuan was embarrassed for them.

Sure enough, the three fox demons still stood on the hilltop, watching with hatred and speaking ruthless words with the intent to harm those inside.

Ji Yuan immediately tried his lighting control skill, which he had not even mastered. He could not gather clouds to produce lighting, but he did succeed in drawing down a bolt of lightning during the thunderstorm. Unfortunately, he had no control over it.

He wanted to directly capture the three foxes, but along his journey, he had felt that Wan Province, which should have been prosperous, felt a little ill. Datong Mountain, which wasn’t all that large of a mountain, was able to directly give rise to three extremely evil fox spirits. It was obviously unlikely that the local land spirit could induce the enlightenment of normal animals.

As it was in the nature of mountains, Datong Mountain attracted thick and heavy energy from all directions; it must have also gathered the evil energy from the vast surrounding area. If the fundamental problem were not solved, Datong Mountain might continue to give rise to evil spirits.

Every profession had its specialties; if he wanted to solve the fundamental problem, he would have to look into the government administration and the people’s livelihood. However, Ji Yuan believed that, as a cultivator, he could still cure some of the symptoms. As long as Datong Mountain was taken care of now, at the very least the people living around it would be in less danger from evil spirits.

At that time, Ji Yuan had been patrolling the mountains, but couldn’t find any defined source of spiritual energy, so he landed in the mountains and tried to use the Spirit Binding technique. Unexpectedly, there really was a spirit that had begun to connect with the earth veins and the mountain land.

That being the case, when Ji Yuan observed him with his supernatural eyes and noted that the other party was not an evil creature, he clearly explained the circumstances involved, and pointed out that this was also an opportunity for the Mountain God himself to further his cultivation.

As a monster who aimed to become a mountain god, he might not really care about matters of right and wrong, but he was certain that this immortal in front of him possessed unfathomable abilities, so he naturally did not dare to disobey. All of this led to the events that transpired with Yin Qing and the others.

After explaining the situation, Ji Yuan didn’t stay for long. Instead, he walked around the region. From the sky, aside from a lot of mulberry trees, he couldn’t see anything that would foment discontent.

As for divination, he could try to calculate the fate of a single person or event if he had some information, but it was not possible to do so when it came to general human Qi. He didn’t know whether it was possible for others to do so, but Ji Yuan himself was certainly not capable of it. If he wanted to understand the situation clearly, he could only ask someone involved and knowledgeable, such as his old friend Yin Zhaoxian, who had been an official in Wan Province for several years.

Although Lishun Prefecture was not the capital prefecture of Wan Province, it was definitely one of the top three prefectures with regards to Wan Province’s silk weaving industry, as well as its economy in general.

Flying through the air, he could see mulberry fields stretching on all over the prefecture along the way. Beside them were silk-weaving factories and silk dyeing workshops. It all seemed to be quite prosperous.

The capital city of Lishun Prefecture was not small. After Ji Yuan had landed and entered the city, he did not bother wandering around. Instead, he asked for directions and went straight to the prefecture’s government office.

When meeting someone he knew well, Ji Yuan naturally stopped concealing his eyes.

When his eyelids were half-open, very few people could see his eyes clearly when they weren’t paying attention, but the officers and officials would inspect him carefully if he wanted to see the prefect. When they heard that the visitor had traveled from Ji Province to Lishun Prefecture, they officers did not dare delay, and immediately went to inform their superior.

. . .

“The visitor calls himself Ji Yuan?”

Yin Zhaoxian had gone out in the morning to inspect the implementation of the latest government decrees in several of the surrounding villages. At this moment, he happened to be in the bedroom with his wife. He was surprised when he heard the officer arrive.

“Yes, Sir.1 He looks gentle and polite, but his eyes are strange — they’re just gray and white.”

“Yes, yes, that’s Mr. Ji! Mr. Ji came alone?”

Yin Zhaoxian and his wife naturally hoped that Yin Qing would have come with him, but the officer shook his head.

“There was only one person.”

“Husband, go see Mr. Ji. Don’t keep him waiting!”

Yin Zhaoxian squeezed his wife’s hand and nodded.

“Okay, my wife,2 rest well. I’m going to meet Mr. Ji. I guess Qing’er will be here soon. There’s no way Mr. Ji wouldn’t have brought him along.”

“Mhm!”

Madam Yin, visibly pregnant, lay on the bed, covered in a quilt, and nodded with a smile on her face.

They hadn’t seen Yin Qing for a long time. As parents, they missed him very much.

Ji Yuan had been waiting for some time in the reception room at the back of the building, tea in hand. Before he finished his cup, he heard hurried footsteps from outside. Most importantly, he could sense an unmistakable aura of righteousness in his supernatural eyes, so strong it was almost visible through the walls and door.

Said talented, righteous individual bowed and greeted him as soon as he entered through the door.

“Mr. Ji! You’ve finally come to Wanzhou. I have been waiting for you to visit for a long time! All, all of you may be excused.”

“Yes.”

At his words, the two servants beside him bowed and walked away. Ji Yuan set down his teacup, stood up with a smile, and bowed teasingly.

“Hello Master Yin, oh no, I mean Prefect Yin!”

“Oh, Mr. Ji, stop laughing at me. It’s really not easy to be an official! By the way, where is Qing’er? Didn’t he come with you?”

Ji Yuan gestured to a seat at the table, picked up the teapot, and poured Yin Zhaoxian a steaming cup of tea, as if it were his own home. Of course, the latter did not stand on ceremony, and sat down beside him.

“Yin Qing and his three classmates are traveling together. I estimate that they will be here in three to five days.”

“Oh, that’s good, that’s good.”

Yin Zhaoxian stroked his beard and nodded.

Ji Yuan took a good look at this friend. He hadn’t seen him for only a few years, but he already had some white hairs. It seemed that he was indeed working hard.

“You, the Great Prefect Yin, are the second star scholar in Dazhen’s history who has passed all the imperial examinations in first place. You are a popular figure among both the court and the general public. In your hometown, you have even been called the human incarnation of the Star of Literature.3 How can there be anything that troubles you?”

Yin Zhaoxian waved his hand awkwardly. Even though he had long become accustomed to his current official position, this identity felt strange in front of Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, please stop joking, I have many troubles. Although Wan Province is prosperous on the surface, it is actually very corrupt… Ah, let me tell you— before I took office, particularly in the past seven or eight years, over 70% of the farmland had been turned into mulberry fields, and 90% of the profits are concentrated in the hands of the wealthy families and their descendants. How can the common people live on what little is left? I am their presiding official… Oh, it makes me so mad!”

“Clap!”

Yin Zhaoxiang slammed his hand on the coffee table as he spoke. This was the first time in Ji Yuan’s memory that he had seen seen Master Yin so angry.
