T/n: This Does Not Sound Like a Fun Birthday Party

Chapter 239: Using Force to Suppress “People”

In fact, many of the surrounding imperial guards stationed around the outside of the field or standing on the four corners of the main platform were also apprehensive at the thunder and lightning in the sky, particularly since they had long weapons pointing upwards at rest.

Yan Chang and a few officials from the Imperial Astronomical Department and the Ministry of Rites also hid in some of the peripheral buildings, looking at the high platform under the thunder and lightning from a distance.

Because of his offering of the mooncake, Yan Chang seemed to be highly valued by the emperor at this point in time, so he had more say among the officials in charge of the Land and Water Assembly.

“Lord Yan, with all this thunder and lightning, I’m afraid it’s going to rain soon. What about those experts above?”

Someone asked Yan Chang a little worriedly. The latter frowned and looked towards the high platform.

“Those who have reported themselves to be simple ordinary monks and Taoists, who have come to pray and chant sutras, can go off the stage to take shelter from the rain and rest. As for those who have reported themselves to hold magical powers or be experts, a little wind and rain will naturally not be a problem for them.”

Hearing what Yan Chang said, the officials around him who were jointly responsible for this matter looked at each other, and roughly understood his intentions.

“Even the imperial guards on and around the platform will inevitably suffer together.”

“Then order all the imperial guards on the stage to retreat to the back. It would be better to get wet from the rain than to be struck by lightning.”

When Yan Chang said this, he was facing the deputy commander of the Outer Court Palace, who was also the deputy commander of the Outer Court Palace Imperial Guard. In terms of official rank, his position was actually higher than that of Yan Chang, but in matters of ritual, the Imperial Guard could only serve as assistants.

“Haha, Lord Yan is right. All my comrades have learned martial arts; with their armor, it won’t be a problem to get caught in the rain for half a day.”

As he spoke, the deputy commander sent orders out to several of the soldiers nearby, who drew their swords and began to move, marching around the platform in all four directions, then going up to deliver the order.

Then, in less time than it took to drink a cup of tea, all the imperial guards and warriors on the Assembly platform began to walk down the steps from all four sides in an orderly manner. In the end, only a few thousand experts remained on the entire Assembly platform, each continuing to perform and pray in their own way.

As time passed, the sky gradually grew darker. The storm clouds in the sky moved slowly, gradually covering the entire capital city of the Capital Prefecture.

. . .

“Crack… Boom… rumble…”

Lightning illuminated the face of Emperor Yuan De. At this moment, he looked up at the dark cloudy sky under the eaves of one of the palaces.

Today was the beginning of the Land and Water Assembly, which could also be considered the beginning of his birthday celebration. In nine days, Emperor Yuan De would select an individual to confer the title of “Heavenly Master,” then invite the new Heavenly Master to participate in his longevity banquet.

In addition to the eunuchs and guards, there were also several princes who had accompanied the emperor to look at the sky. However, there were no ministers or concubines.

“Qing’er.”

“Your son is here!”

Emperor Yuan De called out, and Prince Wu, who was beside him, immediately bowed in response.

“You can see that there is lightning and thunder on the day of the Land and Water Assembly’s convocation. Are the Heavens warning me?”1

“This… Your son… Your son doesn’t know either!”

Wu Wen wouldn’t have dared to answer this kind of question no matter how ignorant of decorum he was. The old emperor glanced at him, and then looked at his third son, Yang Hao.

“What do you think, Hao’er?”

Prince Jin frowned and looked at his father, then at the sky.

“Boom…”

The thunder was so fierce that thousands of clouds were illuminated and the darkness was suppressed.

“Father, there is no need to worry too much. Wind, rain, lightning, and thunder are all common phenomena in this season. It would be more abnormal to have summer snow or winder thunder.”

The old emperor just looked at him, but didn’t say much. Then the turned to his other sons, all of whom seemed as though they were thinking, ‘don’t pick me.’

“Crack….crack…crack… Boom… Boom…”

Suddenly, a burst of rapid lightning struck in the east, and the dense, loud noises startled everyone, including the old emperor.

Within the span of a few breaths, the heavy rain finally began to fall with a loud clatter.

. . .

Far away on the Assembly platform in on the eastern side of the city, some evil and demonic cultivators had only just woken up from a trance at this moment.

In the center, a skinny man with a rat-like face stared blankly at a spot just ten zhang away from him. Just now, several thunderbolts had struck at the same time and hit a woman there, just now turning her into a charred corpse, still emitting black smoke.

Many of the “experts” around were so frightened that they collapsed in the rain. The platform, which had originally been filled with the noisy sounds of praying, chanting, and dancing, was now completely silent.

“This is no ordinary lightning… This is a Lightning Technique! Someone is controlling the Lightning-!”

The thin man with the rat-like face shouted in a panic.

“Someone is controlling…”

“Crack…crack…Boom… Boom…”

The man could no longer utter the second half of his sentence as six or seven lightning bolts hit him almost simultaneously, leaving him no time to react.

The most famous aspect of the Lightning Control Technique was not its power, but its almost unavoidable speed. Compared to ordinary Heavenly Lightning, the Lightning Control Technique was full of spiritual energy and true intent. Coupled with the unusual current state of these evil cultivators, its power was even more outstanding.

Some experts who were purely mortals or mortal warriors, here to fill in the numbers, were the first to start panicking. Aside from the very few who had been paralyzed by fright, most of them crawled towards the edge of the platform.

“Aaah! It’s killing people!”

“Run… someone has been struck!”

“Let’s go, money is not as important as life.”

“Don’t push, don’t push…”

More than 90% of the thousands of people were either ordinary people or people from the prefecture’s underworld, who all wished they could grow a few more legs in their haste to get down from the platform. Among them were also some practitioners who had nothing to do with Dazhen’s fate, or had no evils spirits within themselves.

“Boom…”

“Boom…”

Lightning occasionally struck, and though it didn’t hit a person every time, it still struck more than a dozen people as the group fled.

Under these circumstances, the imperial guards below the stage, intimidated by the might of the heavens, moved away from the stage and looked for places to shelter from the rain, so they did not stop these “experts,” who were supposed to stay on stage, from coming down.

Some monks who had escaped safely into the crowd felt very fortunate. Once they were far away from the Assembly platform, the oppressive feeling of fear suddenly subsided. When they looked back, they found that the entire platform seemed to be a hazy gray.

There were at least hundreds of people on the platform who didn’t escape together. It wasn’t that they didn’t want to escape, but rather many of them did not dare to act rashly, or simply could not act.

At this moment, heavy rain continued to fall from the sky. Although the rain flowed away, the Assembly platform under everyone’s feet was covered in a thin layer of shallow water, like a layer of pure glass.

Human figures and strange shadows were reflected on the water surface. Some of the ones standing on the platform looked like human beings, but their reflections in the water revealed them to be monsters.

This, however, was still not the key point: most crucially, as the rain continued to hit the glass-like mirror surface, creating ripples, the shining words below revealed their true colors.

The words carried a soft glow, like endless moonlight blooming in a mirror, or like the bright moon in the rain.

It gave off an extremely powerful aura, and the light shone like magic through the haze of rain. All the remaining hundreds of people felt as if they were being weighed down by something enormous, and could only barely stand.

“Haha… The reflection in the mirror reverses both appearances!”

Ji Yuan was sitting at a tea stall several hundred zhang away from the platform, with his back to the table, his legs crossed, and his hands on his knees. He looked at the faint light in the direction of the platform with a smile on his face and a calm expression.

Since he had mastered the Heaven Rending Sword, Ji Yuan had made great progress in his comprehension of his own inner world, and in the potential applications of his power.

He was very satisfied with this method of taking advantage of the situation. The moonlight, the Edict, the energy of humanity, and the evil spirits of the demons themselves were all indispensable to this technique. The stronger the various energies, the stronger the effect.

Ji Yuan thought, without trying to be modest, that this move was quite ingenious, even amidst the mysterious world of cultivation.

As soon as the rain fell, the entire Assembly platform became akin to a glass mirror. Hundreds of words of the Edicts displayed the essence of the Tao. Combined with the previous entanglement of the energy of humanity and the blooming light of moonlight, it had produced a “mirror-reversal” effect.

The Qi of the revealed demons and monsters onstage had been reflected, and flipped; although their bodies were standing on the platform at this very moment, their Qi had been suppressed under the platform. That was why the water waves could reflect their true bodies. It wasn’t some supernatural power inherent to the mirror itself, but rather just the manifestation of their Qi.

As long one did not have the spiritual power or supernatural force to overturn the enormous stone platform, or to see through this level of cultivation, one would be entangled in the Qi and in the heart, and would not be able to move. In addition, their spiritual foundation would also be suppressed. The more one connected emotionally, the more frightened one became, the more one would be suppressed, unable to stand up.

Of course, there might be unpredictable methods of escape that could help them break free.

“Creak… Creaak…”

A strong man, muscles bulging all over his body, grit his teeth and stood upright.

“Hooh… haah…”

With a “bang,” one of his legs bent at the knees and slammed down on the platform, his whole body trembling and struggling violently. Then the other leg also slammed down into the stone platform with a “bang.” Strangely, the surface of the water only rippled, but there were no splashes.

“Roar…”

The other old man next to him roared in the sky. His face was ferocious, and his bones made creaking sounds. With his demonic aura rising into the sky, the tried his best to take a half a step forward.

As a result, endless lightning fell.

“Crack…Crack…Boom…”

The old beggar couldn’t help but place his hands over the young beggar’s eyes, fearing that he would be blinded by the lightning.

“Tsk tsk tsk… why not just kneel down obediently?”

Of the entire scene, the two beggars were the most relaxed. Not only was their energy free, but the old beggar also had a calm state of mind that could see through the current happenings.

However, the old beggar was secretly shocked in his heart. After all, he had misjudged; he had obviously taken the decree quite seriously, but hadn’t anticipated the effects to be so unexpected.

“Roooar–“

“Aarooooar—“

Amidst the thunder in the sky, the faint and indistinct roar of a dragon could be heard, proclaiming the dragon’s excitement above the clouds.

The various soldiers and officials of Dazhen who had just hidden in various buildings looked at the high platform in horror. Even though they saw a large number of experts fleeing, they couldn’t help but gain a small understanding in their minds as they saw lightning striking the platform from time to time.

‘Is there anyone else up there? ’

The old beggar looked at the sky. Having seen so many tricks, he decided not to stay on the stage any longer. He took the little beggar’s hand and walked off the platform.

During this process, the “experts” who were unable to move one after another looked at him in surprise, pleading, or anger. There were some who begged, while many cursed him due to misunderstandings. Some even wanted to help the old beggar, but as soon as they were distracted and focused on using their spiritual energy, they were immediately pinned down on the stage and unable to move.

The little beggar had never seen such a terrifying scene of chaotic demons in his life. Looking at the reflections of some of the “experts” on the ground, they were all essentially monsters. He was so frightened that he kept shrinking beside the old beggar.

However, the little beggar was not stupid: Grandpa Lu was capable, and he had long understood that, looking at the current situation, these monsters and demons wouldn’t be able to harm anyone.

“Grandpa Lu…Are they all going to die?”

“Hey, that’s not something an old beggar like me can decide. Of course, it’s certainly not something these beings can decide, either.”

It’s worth noting that the Emperor (unsurprisingly) consistently uses 寡人 (guǎrén), a first person pronoun specifically for royalty and nobility. It’s kind of like the “royal We” (us/we) in English, but that would be confusing in translation so I use I/me

Chapter 240: All Gone

It wasn’t uncommon for lightning to strike the platform in heavy rain. With the rumbling of thunder and the blinding lightning, no one dared to check the situation up on the platform, let alone even get close to it.

What was even more strange was that, during the thunderstorm, people could hear beast roars and terrifying screams from the platform, making it even more difficult for people to approach.

Although the imperial guard of the Yang world did not dare to guard the area around the platform, the gods of the various divisions of the City God of the Capital Prefecture and the underworld officials had already blocked some entrances and exits both inside and outside the stages.

Naturally, they didn’t need to go up on the platform to catch people. Apart from keeping an eye on a couple in particular, just to be on the safe side, they would also check the “experts” that had escaped the platform one by one to determine if there were any evil ones who had managed to sneak away among them.

As for those innocent practitioners who really just wanted to take this opportunity to attain the title of “Heavenly Master,” as long as they were not entangled with any hostility or resentment, then they could even turn a blind eye to the rogue spirits and monsters for the time being. At the very least, they could safely get through this next nine-day period of the Ten Gatherings.

Originally, they would never let these spirits and monsters go, but this time, the immortals, monsters, and gods of the realm gathered together, and the Capital Prefecture’s underworld was happy to afford them this courtesy.

In fact, there were others like this back when Emperor Zheng Yuan had been seeking immortality. They had shallow skill in cultivation, and even no hope of progressing, so they used trickery to fool people and try to reduce their karma with the heavy imperial aura, while at the same time trying to use the emperor’s decree to gain a title.

Then as long as they could find a chance to escape, they could work hard to improve their practice.

However, this kind of thing was also very risky. Royal extravagance was the most “poisonous” trend in the world. Even if only experiencing it for a short period of time, such practitioners with shallow concentration could easily lose themselves.

But understanding was one thing, reality was another. Even knowing that it was very dangerous, but there were still people who couldn’t resist the temptation. Everyone could claim that they would be the “exception,” but if one truly was the “exception,” with such character and perseverance that they could truly be titled and escape, then how could their path of cultivation possibly require this kind of method to turn things around?

In contrast, it was the spirits and monsters who had not yet gained high achievements, but persisted in practicing hard, who were more likely to seize this opportunity. If they could be granted a title, the first thing they would consider was how to find a chance to slip away without hesitation. Most of them wouldn’t dare stay in the city, knowing that it was under the underworld’s jurisdiction.

Of course, in addition to these individuals with shallow cultivation, there would inevitably be some masters hiding in the crowd. After all, the formation on the Assembly platform only targeted evil; if they were righteous, they could naturally leave.

All parties of Dazhen, including Ji Yuan, couldn’t tolerate evil beings stirring up trouble. As for the righteous people, as long as they didn’t act outrageously, they would only be startled. However, since they had come to participate in the Ninth Heaven and Ten Gatherings, they might not be scared away, just like the old beggar.

. . .

In a teahouse about two li away from the platform, more than a dozen experts who had left the platform sheltered from the rain. There were also some imperial guards present.

Some sore their soaked clothes, while others simply took off their wet clothes and were shirtless, without keeping up the image of an “expert.” However, the Imperial Guard would not take off their armor, even if the inner lining was wet.

As they sheltered from the rain, everyone’s attention was focused in the direction of the distant platform. Seeing the falling lightning, many people had lingering fears.

“Ah! If I hadn’t run away, I might have been struck to death by lightning!”

“Yeah, didn’t it kill a few people on the platform?”

“Well, maybe the platform was built too high.”

“No, no, no, I think that the way the emperor held the ceremony on the platform was definitely correct!”

“Oh? What do you mean?”

A group of experts gathered together to discuss seemingly unfathomable topics, and even some of the resting imperial guards stretched out their hearing to listen carefully.

“Think about it, why was there a sudden thunderstorm today? I suppose it can only be because they were dissatisfied with the emperor…”

“How dare you! How dare you speak ill of His Majesty!”

Some of the imperial guards stood up immediately, and many of them put their hands on the hilt of their swords, causing some of the experts to turn pale with fright.

“Sir Guard, Sir Guard,1 calm down. How could we dare to disrespect His Majesty? Just listen to me, let me finish!”

Seeing that the imperial guards had relaxed, the man breathed a sigh of relief. The suffocating energy just now was absolutely uncomfortable.

He couldn’t help but think to himself, ‘it is said that the soldiers of the imperial city were unbloodied.2 Who the hell made this rumor? If these imperial guards had never seen blood, how could they have such a strong murderous aura?’

Almost everyone else in the teahouse had their attention attracted by him, and telling stories on such a rainy day created a special atmosphere.

“I didn’t mean to be disrespectful to His Majesty just now, but why do I claim that the Heavens are dissatisfied? Think about it, His Majesty wishes for the dynasty of Dazhen to be forever stable, and he also wishes himself to be wise and renowned for generations…”

Most people understood the second half of the sentence: the emperor wished to reign immortal and enjoy the power and blessings of the country forever.

“It is no exaggeration to say that this is an act against Heaven. Only our wise and powerful emperor can accomplish such a feat!”

The expert who was speaking flattered the emperor slightly,3 before continuing with his own understanding.

“If such a heaven-defying act could not be accomplished, then the Heavens would naturally not have any reaction. However, the might of the Heavens reacted at this point in time, which shows that the method His Majesty is seeking is already defying the Heavens…”4

“That makes sense!”

“Yeah, it does make sense.”

“Master, what immortal land are you from?”

“Well I… Oh, I need to pee. I will take my leave to go and relieve myself.”

The expert felt the urge to urinate, and he had a long story to continue telling, so he simply left people in suspense, then smiled and directly left to the toilet at the back of the teahouse.

The outhouse was located around the back porch of the teahouse. It was particularly dark outside, as the sky was covered in storm clouds. When the expert came back inside after relieving himself, he suddenly felt cold as he passed by the corridor.

This kind of cold was very strange. He looked around, but couldn’t see anything special, and there is no wind outside.

‘Something’s wrong! ’

After thinking about it for a while to try and figure it out, he took out a handful of incense ash from his pocket, dipped it in his own saliva, closed his eyes, and smeared it on his eyelids.

When he opened his eyes again, the expert’s body shook violently.

There were several Yin ghost messengers with faces that were either dark blue or incredibly pale standing next to him. There was also a ghost messenger with a long tongue and thin eyebrows, whose gender he couldn’t distinguish, staring at him with a half-smile on their face.

At this time, an imperial guard happened to come out to the outhouse. When he passed by, he saw the expert curled up against the wall, motionless, and found it a little strange.

“Expert, what’s wrong with you?”

Seeing his pale face, the imperial guard stepped closer and reached out to touch his forehead.

“Oh… Your temperature is quite hot, and you’re sweating a lot. Looks like you caught a cold. I’ll go pee first and them come back to help you.”

The imperial guard withdrew his hand and walked away, muttering, “why is it so cold here…”

The expert there didn’t dare to move. He stood stiffly with an extremely unsightly expression. It was scary to have so many evil spirits surrounding him in broad daylight. He had no idea what they were going to do.

The long-tongued, narrow-eyed ghost scrutinized the expert up and down, and then waved his hand.

“Let’s go, this person is fine.”

The expert only managed to gasp for breath once the ghost messengers had left the teahouse.

. . .

In some key locations and important places in the city, either in the streets or on the roofs, there were one, two, or even three cultivators wearing feathered or long robes. In addition, there were others with profound spiritual powers carrying Greatest Void5 Jade Talismans and riding the wind, hiding in the air.

Compared to the chaotic atmosphere at the Assembly platform, these cultivators all had divine light in in their eyes, and powerful spiritual energy in their bodies. They were all accomplished practitioners, and were some of the immortal cultivators that had left Yuhuai Mountain.

This time, Yuhuai Mountain had mobilized a large force, led by the five great immortals of Yuzhu Peak, who all had profound attainments in Taoism and great spiritual powers. The other cultivators that had left the sect were also worthy of the title of “immortal” in their Mountain. Among them was Qiu Feng, the Yuhuai Mountain cultivator with a close relationship with Ji Yuan, who had a high enough cultivation as well.

Just looking at the shining light in the direction of the platform and the falling lightning in the sky, many people felt that they had no chance to take action.

The heavy rain finally stopped before noon, and the dark clouds gradually dissipated.

Yan Chang and the other court officials, the Imperial Guard, and some of the surrounding experts then carefully went up to the platform to check on the situation after the clouds dispersed.

However, the terrifying scene they had imagined did not appear. The high platform was clean, and no one was left. Even though they had heard some experts say that people had been killed by lightning, there were now no corpses left on the platform.

Those experts who escaped said that there were still hundreds of people up there, but now not a single one was left. This was quite strange.

However, the disappearance the people was still a trivial matter; the failure of the ceremonial ritual would be fatal, as the emperor would surely punish them. This matter could not be concealed. Led by Yan Chang, the responsible officials had no choice but to think about how to smooth this over.

In the palace, before lunch began, Emperor Yuan De received a report from the Ministry of Rites that the whole blessings ceremony was not going smoothly.

Even though he had been mentally prepared, Emperor Yuan De couldn’t help but grow furious. He angrily scolded the Ministry of Rites and the Imperial Astronomical Department for being useless.

However, his anger didn’t last long before it subsided, and he listened to the reporting official’s following words.

Interestingly enough, the official’s subsequent explanation was exactly the same as that of the expert that had been frightened by the ghost messengers in the teahouse.

After their analysis, Emperor Yuan De felt much better.

军 (jūn): of the military, 爷 (yé): grandpa/old gentleman

i.e. had never seen real combat

Lit. “patted the emperor’s horse”

T/n: LOL this guy can BS with the best of them

太虚 (tàixū): great emptiness/the void/heaven/the skies/universe/cosmos

Chapter 241: A Person Who Enjoys All the Glory of the World

The first day of the Assembly did not start smoothly; hundreds of people disappeared out of thin air.1 For the officials in charge of the Assembly, although they just barely managed to get away with it in front of the emperor, they couldn’t make any mistakes in the next few days.

Fortunately, the platforms for the ceremonies honoring the subordinate deities were not that high, and many of them were located in spacious halls, so there should be fewer variables.

The management of these experts was actually relatively loose. Although they were all recorded in the register, their personal freedom was not restricted. If some of them failed to show up before the ceremony began, they would be regarded as having waived their participation.

Indeed, although it was a ceremony to pray for blessings and ward off disasters, it had a certain nature of competition and selection. After all, Emperor Yuan De wished to select masters to be canonized.

But after all, it was a Land and Water Assembly, so this so-called competition could not be a battle. Therefore, in order to reveal their own uniqueness, the method of revealing their “superiority” over others was also a kind of skill.

After the rain stopped, the remaining experts all returned to their inns one after another, following the arrangement of the imperial court.

When most people had left, a young monk with a Khakkhara staff2 stood in front of the steps to the stage, gazing at the entire platform with a solemn expression.

The monk had handsome features, with red lips and white teeth. His skin was white as suet but not feminine. He wore a habit and a bamboo hat. His posture was as straight as the staff in his hand.

“Praise be to the Great Mingwang Buddha…”

“Master, you have quite the handsome appearance!”

A voice suddenly sounded next to him. The monk was so frightened that he couldn’t help but tremble. Although the voice was neutral and gentle, it came too suddenly.

Ji Yuan had been standing next to the young monk for an unknown length of time without him noticing, and spoke with a smile. The monk’s startled reaction was somewhat unexpected, but it was also quite interesting.

The monk looked at Ji Yuan standing beside him and bowed.

“As you say, benefactor, but the body is merely a shell, there is no need to be attached to it.”

“Yes, but when the appearance reaches a high enough level, forcing people not to be attached to it is also a form of attachment.”

Ji Yuan bowed with cupped hands in return and casually spoke of some theory that he had read on the internet in his previous life. In truth, it was also an expression of his true feelings. After all, it was too far-fetched to expect others to regard this monk as ordinary when he looked like this.

The monk was stunned for a moment, then returned the salute to Ji Yuan again.

“Praise be to the Great Mingwang Buddha! The benefactor can see through it clearly, it is me who is too obsessed with appearances.”

The clouds in the sky had almost dissipated at this time, and the sun shone once more in the land of the Capital Prefecture. Ji Yuan looked around and saw some pedestrians appearing one after another. Some of them had heard or seen some of the things that had previously happened on the Assembly platform, and were wondering if there would be something exciting to see.

“Why did the master come to participate in this Land and Water Assembly?”

Ji Yuan asked softly.

“Only to pray for blessings and ward off disasters!”

The monk did not go back to chanting the Buddha’s name, but rather respectfully replied.

Ji Yuan didn’t voice whether he believed it or not, nor did he express any opinion. He just nodded at him, then turned and left.

The monk watched as Ji Yuan disappeared from sight, then looked at the large platform further away. He wiped the fine sweat on his forehead, picked up his staff, and quickly walked away.

‘Let’s hurry back to the inn where I’m staying. ’

. . .

In the next so-called “Ninth Heaven Ten Gatherings” that followed, the people of the Capital Prefecture could not only hear bursts of chanting near the various platforms, but could also often see some experts “performing” on the streets.

Because the venues on each of the sub-platforms were not large, the remaining nine Gatherings were all held in different locations in the capital, with different batches of experts taking turns to show off their greatness. This also made it convenient for some officials and their subordinates, such as the chief secretaries, to carefully observe each expert.

During this process, it was very important to be able to clearly and directly show one’s skills at the Assembly.

At the same time, in order to make it easier to “stand out” at the Land and Water Assembly, some experts would also “unintentionally” reveal their divine power in the capital city of the Capital Prefecture.

Some people chose to curry favor with high-ranking officials during their rest time, some chose to set up stalls in busy streets to tell fortunes, some even went so far as to deliberately behave in extremely strange ways to attract attention.

This is very new experience for the cultivators of Yuhuai Mountain who seldom went down the mountain. In addition, Ji Yuan, the Old Dragon, and the ghosts and gods of the Capital Province were also there to watch the fun.

Ji Yuan was drinking tea at the window seat on the second floor of the Qingye Building. Sitting opposite him was the Dragon Son Ying Feng, and sitting on the other side facing the window was the Dragon Daugther Ying Ruoli.

Downstairs, there was a “Dharma stall” set up by an old expert with the appearance of an immortal. The stall was comprised of a small cart with many notes posted across it.

In short, the notes clearly stated that they could be used to tell fortunes or solve problems, but that it would only work for those who were destined to be helped. If someone didn’t come with gold, the service would not be accepted. It also said that if it didn’t work, the customer would not be charged a single penny; if it did work, but they had no money to pay, a sincere thank you would be accepted.

In short, that was more or less the gist.

Ji Yuan drank his tea while listening to the storytelling coming from the first floor, and sometimes glanced outside.

“Uncle Ji, the old man down below is just a some ordinary man at the end of his life. His cultivation is extremely shallow, at most he has a little aura, some martial arts, and some minor tricks. Why did you choose him? He can’t beat my father!”

The Dragon Son finally couldn’t help but ask. The Dragon Daughter also had a similar expression. At first, the two thought that there might be another hidden master downstairs, so they patiently looked around and observed carefully. The Dragon Son even disguised himself and went to the stall to investigate, but was naturally disappointed.

Ji Yuan and the Old Dragon had made a bet: each chose one or two people of the remaining experts to see whose pick would stand out in the end. The Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter were just here to join in on the fun.

“Hey, the good part hasn’t come yet. Here it is, pay attention.”

While Ji Yuan was talking, a woman and an honest man came up from the street down below, heading straight towards the expert’s stall.

“Sir, this is it!”

“Ooh!”

The man responded with a few greetings, walked to the stall, looked around, and knelt down with a thud. The women next to him also knelt down beside him.

“Thank you, Master, for saving us-!”

The man appeared dull, but his voice was quite loud, which startled the passers-by around him.

The expert behind the booth immediately stood up and walked around the booth to help the two people up.

“Why must the two of you go through all this trouble? Come, quickly, stand up!”

“No, Master, you have been so kind to our family and refused to accept the money that my wife brought. I have no way to repay you but to kneel and thank you!”

“How can I dare accept this, how can I dare accept this? Stand up!”

The expert used the strength in both his hands to pull the man and woman up. The two wanted to kneel down again, but they were still no match for the expert’s strength.

Many people chatted about the scene, wondering what the master had done to make this couple so excited.

“Hey, you won’t accept my money, and you won’t let me kneel. How can we repay your kindness, Master? Fellow villagers, please tell us how we might repay him!”

A curious person among the gathered crowd spoke something like the following sentence:

“What did the Master do to help you?”

“Yes, tell us about it.”

“Just tell us.”

When the man saw that the expert had returned to his stall, he sighed, shook his head, and recounted the previous events that had happened to his wife in a very moving voice, telling them about how the master had helped them resolve their problems. There were even some miracles involved in his recounting of the events, which amazed the surrounding crowd.

Finally, the couple left after profusely expressing their gratitude. After more than half an hour, another old woman came to express her gratitude. She had also been helped without the expert accepting her money. Then a young woman came to express her gratitude, noting that the Master had only asked for ten coins.

After a day’s work, as the expert was closing his stall, apart from simply telling fortunes to curious people, this scene had repeated itself about four or five times, and he had only accepted money once.

When the expert had packed up his stall and left, the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter looked at each other, then the former turned to Ji Yuan.

“Uncle Ji, was I wrong?”

“Haha, no, let’s go, we haven’t finished watching yet.”

Ji Yuan stood up, paid for the tea, and led the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter out to the street. His figure faded as he walked, and soon they arrived behind a sparsely populated shack in a certain neighborhood.

“Hey, Master, how was my performance?”

“Not bad!”

“And mine too, what about me!”

“Here!”

“Thank you, Master, thank you, Master!”

……

When Ji Yuan arrived with the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter, they watched as the common people who had previously come to express their gratitude were now all taking money from the expert, all of which were either small change or five-wen coins.

“Is this okay?”

The Dragon Son stared blankly at this scene, then turned to look at Ji Yuan.

“Uncle Ji, in the mortal world, this can be considered deceiving the emperor, right? Isn’t he afraid of being beheaded?”

Ji Yuan smiled, spoke a single sentence, and turned to leave.

“He is quite similar to Emperor Yuan De.”

The Dragon Son frowned and looked at the Dragon Daughter.

“Little sister, do you understand?”

Ying Ruoli also frowned and thought for a while, then looked again at the expert who was taking money out of his purse. Although he appeared to be immortal, he was also quite thin and old.

“Uncle Ji might be saying that this person is no longer afraid of being beheaded for the sake of a ‘legitimate title.’”

“Uh, then our father will lose?”

The Dragon Daughter glared angrily at the Dragon Son.

“The one our father chose is really talented, how could he lose? I think compared to Uncle Ji, our father is cheating!”

“Then it’s a draw!”

The Dragon Son smiled and turned to leave.

. . .

The 30th day of the ninth lunar month was the birthday of Emperor Yuan De of Dazhen, so this day was also known as the Longevity Festival.

The entire palace was decorated with lights, and palace servants were busy all over the grounds, both inside and outside the palace. The imperial kitchens and other related locations had started preparations for this day long ago.

On this day, all the ministers tried their best to restrain themselves in the morning court. If it was not very important or urgent, they would try their best not to report things that would make the emperor unhappy.3

The discussion phase soon finished, and all the ministers in the hall quieted down.

Emperor Yuan De looked at the old eunuch beside the dragon throne.

“Bring the Experts in.”

The old eunuch bowed slightly and nodded, then took a deep breath and exhaled loudly.

“Announcing, all the great masters of the Land and Water Assembly, now entering the hall!!!”

Outside the hall, the palace servants repeated the announcement loudly.

“Announcing, all the great masters of the Land and Water Assembly, now entering the hall!!!”

After three rounds, under the gaze of the ministers facing the entrance of the hall, a eunuch led a small group of people through the palace gate and into the main hall.

Some of the officials narrowed their eyes, some laughed, some smiled, some snorted, and some were curious.

Among the people who came in were monks and Taoists, young and old, and even an old beggar in tattered clothes. There were sixteen people in total.

The emperor also squinted from his place up high and looked down.

The group stood still and saluted upwards.

“Greeting His Majesty! Long Live Your Majesty, Long Live, Long Live!”

“No need to stand on ceremony!”

“Thank you, Your Majesty!”

He had to admit, upon closer inspection, all the experts were in high spirits with calm expressions. At the very least, no one showed any signs of fear. The most eye-catching one was naturally the old beggar. There was nothing he could do about it, he was just too out of place.

“Good Minister Yan, which expert did you recommend?”

Yan Chang came out from the crowd, holding a jade tablet and bowing.

“Your Majesty, this minister recommended Mr. Lu. This is him!”

The person Yan Chang was pointing at was actually the beggar, which surprised some officials in the court, who had long regarded him as a treacherous minister.

Emperor Yuan De looked at Yan Chang, and then took a closer look at the beggar.

“What spiritual powers do you have?”

The old beggar took a step forward, but instead of being lazy as usual, be bowed to the emperor, and replied something that had nothing to do with the emperor’s question.

“I, an old beggar, decided on a whim to accept two apprentices. One of them would suffer all the hardships of the world since childhood, and the other would enjoy enjoy all the glory of the world when he grows old. I felt that this Land and Water Assembly might be an opportunity. I would like to ask Your Majesty, are you willing to give up your dragon throne?”

“Bold!”

“Outrageous!”

“Mr. Lu, what nonsense are you talking about? Quickly apologize to His Majesty!”

“You dare to offend His Majesty!”

“Shiing…”

“Shiing…”

“Shiiing…”…

The ministers on the side glared angrily. Yan Chang was covered in a cold sweat. Even the experts subconsciously stayed away from this old beggar, and the imperial guards were even more on edge.

Sitting high on the dragon throne, and Emperor Yuan De suppressed his anger and asked coldly.

“So you hold the secret to immortality?”

The old beggar frowned and pondered, then shook his head and answered.

“I know of no path to immortality.”

“Then can you obtain power and wealth that surpasses my position as Emperor, or freedom that surpasses the Emperor’s status as the supreme ruler?”

Originally, he thought that since the old emperor was eager to seek immortality, although his question seemed inappropriate, the other party might at least take it seriously. However, things did not go as he wished. It seemed that this emperor could not tolerate even the slightest disobedience…

The old beggar paled.

“This kind of freedom, I’m afraid not.”

“Oh, then if I step down from the throne, the position of emperor will be vacant, and the country’s affairs will be left unattended. What will happen then?”

The old beggar was expressionless.

“A country can’t be without a ruler for a day, and Dazhen may be able to grow to rival the power of the Heavens. Naturally, the throne will be abdicated to the prince. The emperor is certainly old enough.”

Emperor Yuan De glanced at the princes, and his anger surged. A lot of thoughts ran through his mind. At this moment, he was so furious that he laughed.

“Hahaha… hahahahaha… Yan Chang, is this the person you recommended?”

“Your Majesty! This minister deserves death! This minister deserves death!”

The Head of the Astronomical Department was so frightened that he fell to his knees, kowtowed, and bowed his head, not daring to get up.

T/n: This is why I like Grandpa Lu. He just called the emperor a fossil who should have retired years ago and worse in character than a beggar boy, to his face. Sure, he’s kind of a jerk, but man do I respect the sheer audacity. That being said, he put Yan Chang under fire. So I can’t like him all that much.

禅杖 (chánzhàng): staff of a Buddhist monk, also called a Khakkhara. See manhua chapter 137 for reference

Chapter 242: The End of This Destiny

Although the Astronomical Department Head Yan Chang had been accused of misleading the emperor in the eyes of some of the ministers, in truth his main responsibility was to observe celestial phenomena, calculate the solar terms, and formulate the calendar.

These jobs truly needed to be performed by intelligent people, and often seemed a bit mysterious, but Yan Chang rarely did superfluous things on work days. He was the most Buddhist among the court officials: he did not flatter, nor did he give unsolicited advice.

However, as the Head of the Imperial Astronomy Department, he would inevitably be pushed to the forefront before the emperor when it came to matters like the Land and Water Assembly.

Almost all of the Heads of the Astronomical Department possessed some special qualities; they had been observing celestial phenomena during all four seasons day after day, year after year, they might have been able to glean some of its subtleties. It wasn’t without reason that Yan Chang could feel that the old beggar was special even among the assembled experts, just like he could observe the stars.

But Yan Chang never thought that the old beggar would bring him disaster. He now lay on the ground, not daring to move.

The emperor sneered and pointed a finger downwards.

“Capture the Astronomical Observations Department Head Yan Chang, and this old beggar!”

“Yes!”

At the emperor’s command, the guards around him instantly leapt into action, their advanced martial arts skills giving them explosive momentum.

In an instant, their silhouettes flashes by, and knives had been placed against the old beggar’s neck from three directions: the left, right, and back. He was surrounded by blades.

Two other knives were placed on Yan Chang’s neck. The guards confiscated the jade tablet and detained him with his hands behind his back. His body was like a child at the mercy of the guards, and they pulled him directly up off the floor.

Yan Chang’s face was pale, his heart beating so fast that it felt like it was about to burst out of his chest. His mind was full of the same thought: ‘It’s over, it’s over…’.

“Yan Chang, let me ask you, who suggested that you recommend this old beggar? Was it Prince Wu or Prince Jin?”

The two princes were terrified, and almost simultaneously knelt down and cried out their grievances.

“How could your son dare do such a treasonous thing?”

“Your son was not aware!”

This was truly an unexpected crisis for the two princes, and they hated Yan Chang even more at this moment.

“Ah…”

Yan Chang closed his eyes and sighed quietly, trying to calm himself down, before opening his eyes once more.

“Your Majesty, I recommended him myself. It has nothing to do with Their Two Highnesses. Mr. Lu is a true master, but this minister didn’t know that he would dare say such treasonous words before the court. I recognize my crime…”

Emperor Yuan De snorted coldly and looked at the old beggar. The latter’s expression remained the same, particularly that pair of eyes staring at him. His gaze was very calm, and he felt some inexplicable feelings in his heart.

“Then what about you? Did someone instruct you to do this…”

Emperor Yuan De hesitated for a moment, then continued.

“Or did you truly intend to say that just now?”

Of the experts who had made their way to the sides of the hall, some looked calm but were uneasy in their hearts, while others appeared to be gloating.

The jade-faced1 monk was also among the group of experts. At this moment, he looked at the old beggar, and then at the old emperor, murmured to himself, and sighed.

“Praise be to the Great Mingwang Buddha…”

The old beggar just looked at Emperor Yuan De and did not answer him immediately. Instead, he glanced at Yan Chang, whose face was ashen.

“Hey, Mr. Yan, this old beggar feels a little sorry for you…”

After saying this, the old beggar looked up at the emperor and smiled again. However, he no longer had the same solemnity and respect that he had originally shown in his first question. Instead, he was as cynical as he usually was when he was begging.

“Your Majesty, this matter is all because this old beggar uttered some arrogant words. It’s not Lord Yan’s fault. It has nothing to do with the princes. How about this? Execute me, this old beggar, and it should also dispel His Majesty’s anger.”

Execution?

Yan Chang was now extremely sensitive to this word. Then he realized that it was not him that they were talking about, but rather this old beggar who was asking for his own death.

Although the court officials to either side had many speculations in their hearts, such as the possibility that this might be to protect some mastermind behind the scenes, they did not dare discuss it at this moment, so the front of the hall was very quiet.

The old emperor looked gloomy and uncertain as he stared at the old beggar.

It took a long time for him to speak.

“If you truly have supernatural powers or mysterious magic, show them to me. As long as they are real, I will not only forgive your crime, but also confer a title and reward upon you!”

As soon as these words were spoken, there were whispers of chatter among the court officials. Even Yan Chang looked at the old beggar with hope. He had seen Ji Yuan wielding his sword and dancing in the moonlight, and he had seen the old beggar catch a cat, but from the conversation between the group of masters at that time, it seemed that the cat was also extraordinary.

However, the old beggar shook his head and smiled wryly.

“No need, just execute this old beggar.”

“You……”

The old emperor gripped the armrests of the dragon throne tightly with both hands, leaned forward, and spoke sternly.

“Do you really think I wouldn’t dare to kill you?”

Even though the old beggar had three knives held to his neck, he seemed to be ignoring them. With his hands restrained by the guards, he simply tilted his head and rubbed his shoulder against the blades to scratch an itch.

Even the guards nearby couldn’t help but secretly marvel at the old beggar’s boldness. They grabbed his hands and used their Qi to probe his body, finding that his body was “empty” — he was not a martial arts master.

Once the old beggar finished scratching his itch, he looked at the old emperor with the same expression, and there was even a hint of pity in his eyes.

“Your Majesty, there is no need to worry about killing an old beggar. Your Majesty, please proceed.”

The veins on the back of the emperor’s old hand twitched due to the force of his agitation, and his chest heaved in anger. The way the old beggar looked at him made him furious.

“Hahaha…Okay! Palace Guard, heed my orders. Take this old beggar out to Yongning Street immediately and behead him! As a warning! To everyone!”2

“As the Head of the Astronomical Observations Department in charge of this event, Minister of Ceremonies Yan Chang shall be…”

Yan Chang on the side couldn’t help but tremble, and fine beads of sweat kept oozing out of his forehead.

“Dismissed from his post, and thrown into the Heavenly Prison!”

“Yes Sir!”

“Yes Sir!”

Yan Chang’s whole body seemed to have lost all strength and fell to the ground. The two guards carried him and dragged him out of the hall. He used his remaining strength to look back at the old beggar; the latter walked steadily and was escorted out of the hall by several guards.

After the two “criminals” were escorted out, the hall became silent. The two princes were still kneeling on the ground, too afraid to stand up or raise their heads. They could even feel their father’s eyes on them.

“Hmph, you two, get up!”

“Thank you, Father!”

The two thanked him in unison. When they stood up, they subconsciously glanced at each other, and both saw a lingering fear in each other’s eyes.

After experiencing such an incident, many of the remaining fifteen experts in the hall were trembling a little in fear. The lessons they had learned in the past had played out right before their eyes. The other officials from the Imperial Astronomical Department and the Ministry of Rites who were in charge of the Assembly ceremonies were also very nervous.

As the saying went, an emperor’s order must not be neglected. Since the emperor personally ordered the immediate execution in front of the palace, the guards naturally perked up and immediately escorted the old beggar to the street outside the palace.

There was no need to wait, not even until noon. The palace guards escorted the old beggar, followed by a squadron of imperial guards, and brought him directly to Yongning Street, as if he were a court minister who had committed a crime.

The Imperial Guard, armed with spears and halberds, cleared the way of pedestrians ahead.

“Out of the way, out of the way, don’t block the road!”

“Bystanders, please stay away!”

When many people saw this scene, they knew that someone was going to be beheaded. Immediately, many people followed around the periphery, and even more with less benevolent intentions rushed over upon hearing the news.

“It seems that someone is going to be beheaded over there!”

“Really?”

“Go and see and you’ll find out!”

“Let’s go…”

“Wait for me.”

Watching a beheading was also a special kind of lively spectacle.

“Hey, there seems to be an old beggar in the prison cart?”

“Is it that minister who came out of the prison?”

“No, he’s really a beggar. Look at his clothes, it’s not even a prison uniform!”

“That’s true…”

“This beggar doesn’t seem to be afraid at all?”

“Is he scared?”

……

This scene also attracted some special people, including Ji Yuan and several cultivators from Yuhuai Mountain.

Ji Yuan also followed the crowd of ordinary people who were moving alongside the prison cart, and shouted in the direction of the prison cart with a puzzled voice.

“Mr. Lu, what crime have you committed?”

Only the old beggar’s head was exposed outside the prison cart, and he wore shackles and ankle restraints. He could barely turn around to shout at Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, I offended the emperor in court, and I shall be beheaded!”

Ji Yuan was a little dumbfounded, but at the same time a little curious. If this old beggar didn’t get away, would he really be beheaded?

But before he had time to think about it, two imperial guards immediately came to Ji Yuan and looked at him seriously.

“Who are you and what is your relationship with the criminal?”

“Uh… Gentlemen, please understand that I just drank tea with this old beggar. I have nothing else to do with him!”

Ji Yuan bowed with cupped hands, his demeanor composed, with a calm smile on his face.

The imperial guards looked at each other, but they didn’t make things too difficult for him. They just told him to stay out of the way.

Although the Imperial Guard let Ji Yuan leave, the old beggar did not let Ji Yuan go so easily.

“Mr. Ji~! I heard that beggars can eat a meal before their execution. I’m a little tired, how about you help me out and prepare me a bowl of rice to eat?”

Seeing his look, Ji Yuan smiled, nodded, and turned away.

When Ji Yuan came back, the old beggar was chained to the center of Yongning Street, surrounded from afar by a large group of ordinary people.

“Please make way, please make way, I’m here to serve him his last meal!”

Ji Yuan apologized and squeezed his way forward. When the others heard that it was a last meal for the prisoner, they immediately moved away. Soon, Ji Yuan was able to squeeze past the wall of people.

Several cultivators from Yuhuai Mountain stood together, looking at Ji Yuan and the old beggar with strange expressions.

“Master Uncle, what kind of trick is going on here?”

The old man who had been questioned thought about it carefully, his gaze uncertain.

“Just watch.”

Ji Yuan carried a bowl full of minced pork with rice and sauce. After greeting the surrounding palace and imperial guards, he walked over to the old beggar. By that point, the old beggar’s shackles had been undone, so he could eat by himself.

“Mr. Lu, please enjoy.”

The old beggar took the big bowl with a big smile, thanked him, and started eating with large mouthfuls.

He quickly finished the full bowl of rice. It tasted delicious.

“Buurp…”

After a burp, the old beggar returned the bowl and chopsticks to Ji Yuan.

“Hehe… Mr. Ji, you had better move a little further away, so as not to let this old beggar’s blood stain your clothes for a while. Oh, but you have an immaculate body, so you must not be afraid of this little bit of dirt.”

Ji Yuan took the bowl and chopsticks, shook his head, and walked slowly back to the crowd of spectators outside.

It seemed that the old beggar truly intended to be “beheaded.” This reminded Ji Yuan of the Ghost Mother he had previously captured, who was also able to survive after being beheaded.

“Since you’ve finished eating, let’s be on our way. Don’t blame us for being ruthless, blame yourself for committing a capital crime!”

After the palace guard in front of the hall finished speaking, he personally acted as the executioner: he drew his sword and raised it high, while the old beggar knelt on the ground with his head lowered.

Almost all the onlookers subconsciously shrank their bodies back, and there were gasps and trembling in the crowd.

The next moment, the guard swung his sword.

“Pfft…”

A head rolled down, blood spurted from his neck, and many onlookers shivered.

“Ah…”

“Aaaah…!”

“Hiss…”

Ji Yuan opened his supernatural eyes and looked carefully at the old beggar. Suddenly, he realized something.

After about four or five breaths, something happened that horrified all the onlookers and the executioners.

The headless corpse lying on the ground actually stood up by itself, and staggered forward, feeling around itself.

“Aaah…!”

“A ghost!”

“It’s a zombie, run!”

“Aaaah…!”

“Run…”

“Don’t push me!”

……

All the common people fled in a hurry, and the surrounding Imperial and Palace Guards were all horrified. They couldn’t escape, nor could they move forward, and their bodies were a little stiff…

“Hey, here, here!”

The head that rolled to the side spoke, as if guiding the body to come find itself.

After another moment, the headless body picked up the head and pressed it back on his neck.

“Crack! Crack! Crack…”

The old beggar twisted his neck, making a crackling sound, and then looked at the palace guards with a smile on his face.

“Alas, the fate of this dynasty cannot be easily influenced. The master-disciple destiny between me and the emperor… is no more!”

T/n: JY’s cultivator friends are all some flavor of troll.

i.e. handsome, fair-skinned
